     1→Rails.application.routes.draw do
     2→  root "dashboard#index"
     3→
     4→  # Mount Action Cable for WebSocket connections
     5→  mount ActionCable.server => "/cable"
     6→
     7→  resource :session
     8→  resources :passwords, param: :token
     9→
    10→  get  "invites/:token",        to: "registrations#new",    as: :new_registration
    11→  post "invites/:token/accept", to: "registrations#create", as: :accept_registration
    12→
    13→  get  "auth/:provider/callback", to: "oauth_callbacks#create"
    14→  post "auth/:provider/callback", to: "oauth_callbacks#create"
    15→  get  "auth/failure",            to: "oauth_callbacks#failure"
    16→  resource :change_password, only: [ :show, :update ]
    17→
    18→  resource :settings, only: :show do
    19→    patch :update_profile
    20→    patch :update_password
    21→    patch :toggle_tailscale
    22→    post :generate_token
    23→    delete "revoke_token/:id", action: :revoke_token, as: :revoke_token
    24→  end
    25→
    26→  get  "auth/device",              to: "device_auth#show",     as: :auth_device
    27→  post "auth/device/verify",       to: "device_auth#verify",   as: :auth_device_verify
    28→  get  "auth/device/approve/:id",  to: "device_auth#confirm",  as: :auth_device_confirm
    29→  post "auth/device/approve",      to: "device_auth#approve",  as: :auth_device_approve
    30→
    31→  resources :sandboxes, only: [ :new, :create, :show, :destroy ] do
    32→    member do
    33→      post :start
    34→      post :stop
    35→      post :retry
    36→      get :stats, controller: "dashboard", action: "stats"
    37→      get :card, controller: "dashboard", action: "card"
    38→      post :terminal, controller: "terminal", action: "open"
    39→      get  "terminal/wait", controller: "terminal", action: "wait"
    40→      get  "terminal/status", controller: "terminal", action: "status"
    41→      delete :terminal, controller: "terminal", action: "close"
    42→      post :vnc, controller: "vnc", action: "open"
    43→      get  "vnc/wait", controller: "vnc", action: "wait", as: :vnc_wait
    44→      get  "vnc/status", controller: "vnc", action: "status", as: :vnc_status
    45→      delete :vnc, controller: "vnc", action: "close"
    46→      post :snapshot, controller: "snapshots", action: "create_for_sandbox"
    47→    end
    48→  end
    49→
    50→  resources :snapshots, only: [ :index, :destroy ], param: :name do
    51→    member do
    52→      post :clone
    53→    end
    54→  end
    55→
    56→  get "terminal/auth", to: "terminal#auth"
    57→  get "vnc/auth", to: "vnc#auth"
    58→
    59→  resource :tailscale, only: [], controller: "tailscale" do
    60→    get :show
    61→    post :login
    62→    get :login_status
    63→    get :connected
    64→    patch :update_settings
    65→    delete :disable
    66→  end
    67→
    68→  namespace :admin do
    69→    get "/", to: "dashboard#index", as: :dashboard
    70→    get "system_status", to: "dashboard#system_status"
    71→    resource :settings, only: [ :edit, :update ]
    72→    resources :users
    73→    resources :invites, only: [ :index, :create, :destroy ]
    74→    resources :sandboxes, only: :destroy do
    75→      member do
    76→        post :start
    77→        post :stop
    78→        get :stats
    79→      end
    80→    end
    81→
    82→    # Job monitoring dashboard
    83→    mount MissionControl::Jobs::Engine, at: "/jobs"
    84→
    85→    # Error tracking dashboard
    86→    mount SolidErrors::Engine, at: "/errors"
    87→  end
    88→
    89→  namespace :api do

    91→      member do
    92→        post :start
    93→        post :stop
    94→        post :connect
    95→        post :snapshot
    96→        post :restore
    97→        post :tailscale_connect
    98→        delete :tailscale_disconnect
    99→      end
   100→      resources :routes, only: [ :index, :create, :destroy ], param: :domain, constraints: { domain: %r{[^/]+} }
   101→    end
   102→    resources :snapshots, only: [ :index, :create, :show, :destroy ], param: :name
   103→    resources :users
   104→    resource :status, only: :show
   105→    resource :info, only: :show
   106→    resources :tokens, only: [ :index, :create, :destroy ]

```
# Configure SolidErrors to use session-based authentication (no HTTP Basic Auth needed)
Rails.application.configure do
  config.to_prepare do
    SolidErrors::ApplicationController.class_eval do
      # Skip HTTP Basic auth filter (we use session-based auth instead)
      skip_before_action :authenticate, raise: false

      include Authentication
      before_action :require_authentication
      before_action :require_admin

      private

      def require_admin
        unless Current.user&.admin?
          redirect_to root_path, alert: "Access denied. Admin privileges required."
        end
      end
    end
  end
end
```

    32→
    33→    def request_authentication
    34→      session[:return_to_after_authenticating] = request.url
    35→      redirect_to new_session_path
    36→    end
    37→
    38→    def after_authentication_url
    39→      session.delete(:return_to_after_authenticating) || root_url
    40→    end
    41→
    42→    def start_new_session_for(user)
    43→      user.sessions.create!(user_agent: request.user_agent, ip_address: request.remote_ip).tap do |session|
    44→        Current.session = session
    45→        cookies.signed.permanent[:session_id] = { value: session.id, httponly: true, same_site: :lax }
    46→      end
    47→    end
    48→
    49→    def require_password_change
    50→      return unless Current.user&.must_change_password?
    51→      redirect_to change_password_path, alert: "You must change your password before continuing."
    52→    end
    53→
    54→    def terminate_session
    55→      Current.session.destroy
    56→      cookies.delete(:session_id)
    57→    end
    58→end

/Users/thies/.local/share/mise/installs/ruby/4.0.0/lib/ruby/gems/4.0.0/gems/solid_errors-0.7.0
module SolidErrors
  class ApplicationController < SolidErrors.base_controller_class.constantize
    layout "solid_errors/application"
    protect_from_forgery with: :exception

    http_basic_authenticate_with name: SolidErrors.username, password: SolidErrors.password if SolidErrors.password

    # adapted from: https://github.com/ddnexus/pagy/blob/master/gem/lib/pagy.rb
    OverflowError = Class.new(StandardError)
    class Page
      attr_reader :count, :items, :pages, :first, :last, :prev, :next, :offset, :from, :to

      def initialize(collection, params)
        @count = collection.count
        @items = (params[:items] || 20).to_i
        @pages = [(@count.to_f / @items).ceil, 1].max
        @page = ((page = (params[:page] || 1).to_i) > @pages) ? @pages : page
        @first = (1 unless @page == 1)
        @last = (@pages unless @page == @pages)
        @prev = (@page - 1 unless @page == 1)
        @next = (@page == @pages) ? nil : @page + 1
        @offset = (@items * (@page - 1))
        @from = [@offset + 1, @count].min
        @to = [@offset + @items, @count].min
      end
    end
  end
end
```

> AGENT

The production image is at commit `0edf685` — it predates the solid_errors commit (`6ce1d65`). **The gem isn't in the running image**, so `/admin/errors` 500s because Rails doesn't know about `SolidErrors` at all.

You need to pull and redeploy the latest image (which includes solid_errors and the `d4aeae6`/`6ce1d65` commits), then run migrations. The new database also needs to exist — the postgres init script handles that on fresh installs, but for an existing install you may need to create `sandcastle_production_errors` manually.

Would you like me to handle the deploy, or do you want to run it yourself? The steps would be:

1. Pull latest image: `docker pull ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle:latest`
2. Restart web + worker: `docker-compose up -d sandcastle-web sandcastle-worker`
3. Create the errors DB if missing: `docker exec sandcastle-postgres-1 psql -U sandcastle -c "CREATE DATABASE sandcastle_production_errors;"`
4. Run migrations: `docker exec sandcastle-web bin/rails db:migrate`

[tasks."deploy:dev"]
description = "Run local development with live source mounting"
run = """
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

echo "Starting development environment with live reload..."
docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml up --build

echo ""
echo "Development server running at:"
echo "  - https://localhost:8443 (via Traefik)"
echo "  - http://localhost:8080 (via Traefik)"
echo ""
echo "Changes to Ruby/ERB files will reload automatically"
echo "Tailwind CSS compiles on file changes"
echo ""
echo "Logs: docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml logs -f"
"""

[tasks."deploy:dev:down"]
description = "Stop development docker-compose deployment"
run = "docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml down"

[tasks."deploy:dev:reset"]
description = "Reset development docker-compose environment"
run = """
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

echo "Removing WeTTY containers..."
docker ps -a --filter "name=sc-wetty-" -q | xargs -r docker rm -f || true

echo "Removing Tailscale containers..."
docker ps -a --filter "name=sc-ts-" -q | xargs -r docker rm -f || true

echo "Stopping and removing containers, volumes, and networks..."
docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml down --volumes --remove-orphans

echo ""
echo "Development environment reset complete."
echo "Run 'mise run deploy:dev' to rebuild and start fresh."
"""

[tasks."deploy:local"]
description = "Build image and deploy locally via docker-compose.local.yml"
run = """
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

export BUILD_VERSION=$(git describe --tags --abbrev=0 2>/dev/null || echo "dev")
export BUILD_GIT_SHA=$(git rev-parse --short HEAD 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")
export BUILD_GIT_DIRTY=$([ -z "$(git status --porcelain 2>/dev/null)" ] && echo "" || echo "-dirty")
export BUILD_DATE=$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)

echo "Building sandcastle:local ${BUILD_VERSION} (${BUILD_GIT_SHA}${BUILD_GIT_DIRTY})..."
docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml build

echo "Starting services..."
docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml up

echo ""
echo "Sandcastle running at:"
echo "  - https://localhost:8443 (via Traefik, with terminal support)"
echo "  - http://localhost:8080 (via Traefik, HTTP)"
echo ""
--
[tasks."deploy:local:down"]
description = "Stop local docker-compose deployment"
run = "docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml down"

[tasks."deploy:local:reset"]
description = "Reset local docker-compose environment (stop, remove volumes, rebuild)"
run = """
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

echo "Removing WeTTY containers..."
docker ps -a --filter "name=sc-wetty-" -q | xargs -r docker rm -f || true

echo "Removing Tailscale containers..."
docker ps -a --filter "name=sc-ts-" -q | xargs -r docker rm -f || true

echo "Stopping and removing containers, volumes, and networks..."
docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml down --volumes --remove-orphans

echo ""
echo "Local environment reset complete."
echo "Run 'mise run deploy:local' to rebuild and start fresh."
"""

[tasks."deploy:local:logs"]
description = "Tail logs from local docker-compose deployment"
run = "docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml logs -f"

[tasks."release"]
alias = "release:patch"
description = "Bump patch version, tag, push, and wait for CI to build the release"
run = """
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# Check if HEAD already has a tag
if git describe --exact-match --tags HEAD 2>/dev/null; then
  echo "ERROR: Current commit already has a tag. Make new commits before releasing."
  exit 1
fi

# Get the latest tag
latest=$(git describe --tags --abbrev=0 2>/dev/null || echo "v0.0.0")
echo "Current version: $latest"

# Parse and bump patch
```

> AGENT

The image bundles without dev gems (`BUNDLE_WITHOUT=development`), so switching `RAILS_ENV` to `development` would break things. The cleanest fix is to set `config.consider_all_requests_local = true` in production when a local env var is present:

ActiveRecord::Schema[7.1].define(version: 1) do
  create_table "solid_errors", force: :cascade do |t|
    t.text "exception_class", null: false
    t.text "message", null: false
    t.text "severity", null: false
    t.text "source"
    t.datetime "resolved_at"
    t.string "fingerprint", limit: 64, null: false
    t.datetime "created_at", null: false
    t.datetime "updated_at", null: false
    t.index ["fingerprint"], name: "index_solid_errors_on_fingerprint", unique: true
    t.index ["resolved_at"], name: "index_solid_errors_on_resolved_at"
  end

  create_table "solid_errors_occurrences", force: :cascade do |t|
    t.integer "error_id", null: false
    t.text "backtrace"
    t.json "context"
    t.datetime "created_at", null: false
    t.datetime "updated_at", null: false
    t.index ["error_id"], name: "index_solid_errors_occurrences_on_error_id"
  end

  add_foreign_key "solid_errors_occurrences", "solid_errors", column: "error_id"
end

# frozen_string_literal: true

module SolidErrors
  #
  # Rails generator used for setting up SolidErrors in a Rails application.
  # Run it with +bin/rails g solid_errors:install+ in your console.
  #
  class InstallGenerator < Rails::Generators::Base
    source_root File.expand_path("templates", __dir__)

    def add_solid_errors_db_schema
      template "db/errors_schema.rb"
    end

    def configure_solid_errors
      insert_into_file Pathname(destination_root).join("config/environments/production.rb"), after: /^([ \t]*).*?(?=\nend)$/ do
        [
          "",
          '\1# Configure Solid Errors',
          '\1config.solid_errors.connects_to = { database: { writing: :errors } }',
          '\1config.solid_errors.send_emails = true',
          '\1config.solid_errors.email_from = ""',
          '\1config.solid_errors.email_to = ""',
          '\1config.solid_errors.username = Rails.application.credentials.dig(:solid_errors, :username)',
          '\1config.solid_errors.password = Rails.application.credentials.dig(:solid_errors, :password)'
        ].join("\n")
      end
    end
  end
end
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/db/errors_schema.rb",
  "content": "# This file is the source Rails uses to define the solid_errors schema when running\n# bin/rails db:schema:load. When creating a new database, run bin/rails db:prepare\n# to create the database and load this schema.\n\nActiveRecord::Schema[8.1].define(version: 1) do\n  create_table \"solid_errors\", force: :cascade do |t|\n    t.text \"exception_class\", null: false\n    t.text \"message\", null: false\n    t.text \"severity\", null: false\n    t.text \"source\"\n    t.datetime \"resolved_at\"\n    t.string \"fingerprint\", limit: 64, null: false\n    t.datetime \"created_at\", null: false\n    t.datetime \"updated_at\", null: false\n    t.index [\"fingerprint\"], name: \"index_solid_errors_on_fingerprint\", unique: true\n    t.index [\"resolved_at\"], name: \"index_solid_errors_on_resolved_at\"\n  end\n\n  create_table \"solid_errors_occurrences\", force: :cascade do |t|\n    t.integer \"error_id\", null: false\n    t.text \"backtrace\"\n    t.json \"context\"\n    t.datetime \"created_at\", null: false\n    t.datetime \"updated_at\", null: false\n    t.index [\"error_id\"], name: \"index_solid_errors_occurrences_on_error_id\"\n  end\n\n  add_foreign_key \"solid_errors_occurrences\", \"solid_errors\", column: \"error_id\"\nend\n"
}

Done. After rebuilding (`mise run deploy:local:reset && mise run deploy:local`), the `migrate` service will create the `sandcastle_development_errors` database and load `db/errors_schema.rb` into it, fixing the 500. You'll also get the web-console on error pages.

> DEVELOPER

what happend?

> AGENT

The `deploy:local:reset` wiped the `traefik-data` volume, so a new mkcert CA was generated inside the container. Your browser no longer trusts it. This happens every reset.

Let me check how the cert is generated so we can understand how to fix it permanently:

> TOOL

  "description": "Find mkcert/TLS related files"
}
```

   198→      env,
   199→      "mkcert",
   200→      "-cert-file", cert_path,
   201→      "-key-file",  key_path,
   202→      host, "*.#{host}", "localhost", "127.0.0.1", "::1"
   203→    ) or raise Error, "mkcert certificate generation failed for #{host}"
   204→
   205→    ca_source = File.join(caroot, "rootCA.pem")
   206→    ca_dest   = File.join(cert_dir, "rootCA.pem")
   207→    FileUtils.cp(ca_source, ca_dest) if File.exist?(ca_source) && File.expand_path(ca_source) != File.expand_path(ca_dest)
   208→
   209→    Rails.logger.info("RouteManager: mkcert certificate written to #{cert_path}")
   210→  end
   211→
   212→  def custom_cert_configured?
   213→    cert_path = File.join(DATA_DIR, "traefik", "certs", "custom-cert.pem")
   214→    key_path = File.join(DATA_DIR, "traefik", "certs", "custom-key.pem")
   215→    File.exist?(cert_path) && File.exist?(key_path)
   216→  end
   217→
   218→  def config_path(sandbox)

> AGENT

The `deploy:local:reset` wiped the Docker volume, regenerating the mkcert CA inside the container. Since that CA was never trusted by your system, the browser rejects the cert.

The fix: generate the cert on the **host** (where mkcert is already trusted) and mount it into the container so it survives resets.

tool_use Write
id: toolu_01Cnx5qyLDKbvFbmampQAucc
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/scripts/generate-local-cert.sh",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -euo pipefail\n\n# Generate a locally-trusted TLS certificate for deploy:local (dev.sand).\n# The cert is stored in .local/certs/ and bind-mounted into the container,\n# so it survives docker-compose resets.\n\nCERT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")/..\" && pwd)/.local/certs\"\n\nif ! command -v mkcert &>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"Error: mkcert is not installed\"\n  echo \"Install with: brew install mkcert && mkcert -install\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# Ensure the mkcert CA is trusted by the system\nmkcert -install\n\nmkdir -p \"$CERT_DIR\"\n\necho \"Generating locally-trusted certificate for dev.sand...\"\nmkcert \\\n  -cert-file \"$CERT_DIR/cert.pem\" \\\n  -key-file  \"$CERT_DIR/key.pem\" \\\n  dev.sand \"*.dev.sand\" localhost 127.0.0.1 ::1\n\necho \"\"\necho \"Certificate written to: $CERT_DIR\"\necho \"It will be used automatically by deploy:local.\"\n"
}
```

Perfect! I have gathered enough information to provide a comprehensive analysis. Let me now create a detailed summary report.

## Summary: SSH Host Port Bindings (2201-2299) Analysis

Based on my exploration of the Sandcastle codebase, here's a detailed breakdown of how SSH host ports are currently used and what could potentially work without them:

---

### 1. **How WeTTY Connects to Sandboxes**

**Current approach (Docker internal networking):**
- WeTTY containers (`sc-wetty-{user}-{sandbox}`) are placed on the `sandcastle-web` bridge network
- WeTTY connects to sandboxes **directly by container name** on port 22 (internal Docker DNS), not via the host port
- SSH command in WeTTY: `ssh -p 22 {user}@{sandbox.full_name}` (e.g., `ssh -p 22 alice@alice-mybox`)
- The private SSH key is copied into WeTTY via base64-encoded `docker exec`

**File:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/terminal_manager.rb` lines 222-230

**Conclusion:** WeTTY **does NOT use host ports** — it uses Docker internal networking. Host ports are irrelevant here.

---

### 2. **How Go CLI Connects to Sandboxes**

**Current approach (host ports only):**
- CLI calls `/api/sandboxes/{id}/connect` endpoint to get connection info
- API returns `ConnectInfo` struct with `host` (SANDCASTLE_HOST env var), `port` (the ssh_port from DB), `user`, and optional `tailscale_ip`
- CLI then uses those values to `ssh -p {port} {user}@{host}` 

**Files:**
- `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/vendor/sandcastle-cli/api/types.go` lines 33-39 (ConnectInfo struct)
- `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/vendor/sandcastle-cli/cmd/connect.go` lines 51-61 (uses ConnectInfo.Host and ConnectInfo.Port)
- `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/sandbox_manager.rb` lines 535-558 (connect_info method)

**Conclusion:** CLI **requires host ports** (or Tailscale IP if enabled). This is the primary use case for the 2201-2299 range.

---

### 3. **How VNC Connects to Sandboxes**

**Current approach (Docker internal networking):**
- NoVNC containers (`sc-vnc-{user}-{sandbox}`) are placed on the `sandcastle-web` bridge network
- NoVNC connects to sandboxes directly by container name on port 5900 (VNC protocol), not via host port
- Container creation command: `["--vnc", "{sandbox.full_name}:5900", "--listen", "6080"]`
- VNC **does NOT use host ports**

**File:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/vnc_manager.rb` lines 181, 240-243

**Conclusion:** VNC **does NOT use host ports** — uses Docker internal networking.

---

### 4. **Docker Networks for Sandbox Containers**

**Current approach:**
- **Primary:** Sandbox containers are only accessible via host port bindings (`PortBindings` in Docker API)
- **Secondary (optional):** Sandboxes can be connected to additional networks:
  - `sandcastle-web` network (by TerminalManager and VncManager when opening terminal/VNC)
  - User's Tailscale bridge network `sc-ts-net-{username}` (by TailscaleManager if enabled)
- Sandbox containers are **NOT created on the sandcastle-web network by default** — they're connected only on-demand

**Files:**
- `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/sandbox_manager.rb` lines 67-69 (port bindings)
- `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/terminal_manager.rb` lines 163-176 (connects sandbox to sandcastle-web)
- `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/vnc_manager.rb` lines 140-153 (connects sandbox to sandcastle-web)

**Conclusion:** Sandboxes rely **primarily on host port binding** for accessibility outside Docker. They're only added to named networks on-demand.

---

### 5. **What `sandcastle connect` CLI Command Does**

**Exact flow:**
1. CLI resolves sandbox name via `findSandboxByName(client, name)`
2. If sandbox status is "stopped", auto-starts it
3. Calls `client.ConnectInfo(sandbox.ID)` → `POST /api/sandboxes/{id}/connect`
4. API calls `SandboxManager.new.connect_info(sandbox: @sandbox)` which:
   - Returns `host` from env var `SANDCASTLE_HOST` (or "localhost")
   - Returns `port` from `sandbox.ssh_port` (the host port)
   - Returns `user` from `sandbox.user.name`
   - If Tailscale enabled and IP available, overrides host/port with Tailscale IP/22
5. CLI waits for SSH to be ready on that host:port
6. Executes SSH with tmux: `ssh -p {port} {user}@{host} -t 'tmux new-session -A -s main'`

**Files:**
- `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/vendor/sandcastle-cli/cmd/connect.go` (all flow)
- `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/sandbox_manager.rb` lines 535-558 (connect_info logic)

**Conclusion:** `sandcastle connect` depends entirely on either the host port OR Tailscale IP.

---

### 6. **API Response for Sandbox Connection Info**

**Endpoint:** `POST /api/sandboxes/{id}/connect`

**Response structure (`ConnectInfo`):**
```
{
  host: string,          // SANDCASTLE_HOST or "localhost"
  port: int,             // ssh_port (2201-2299)
  user: string,          // sandbox.user.name
  command: string,       // Pre-formatted SSH command
  tailscale_ip: string   // (optional) If Tailscale enabled and IP available
}
```

**File:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/vendor/sandcastle-cli/api/types.go` lines 33-39

---

### 7. **Existing SSH Proxy/Jump Host Setup**

**Current status:** No SSH proxy or jump host setup exists in the codebase. All connections are direct to the host:port or directly to the Tailscale IP.

---

## What Uses Host SSH Ports vs. What Doesn't

| Component | Uses Port 2201-2299 | Alternative | Notes |
|-----------|:------------------:|------------|-------|
| **Go CLI** (`sandcastle connect/ssh`) | ✅ YES | Tailscale IP if enabled | Primary user-facing use case |
| **WeTTY (web terminal)** | ❌ NO | Docker internal DNS | Uses `sandcastle-web` bridge network |
| **VNC (browser-based)** | ❌ NO | Docker internal DNS | Uses `sandcastle-web` bridge network |
| **API endpoint `/connect`** | ✅ YES (response) | Tailscale IP if enabled | Returns port in JSON response |
| **Port allocation logic** | ✅ YES | — | `find_free_ssh_port()` in SandboxManager |

---

## Key Findings: Elimination Feasibility

### Could Be Eliminated

**✅ For WeTTY and VNC:** These already work without host ports via Docker internal networking. They could function entirely independently.

### Would Still Need It (or Alternative)

**⚠️ For CLI users:** The host port is critical for:
- `sandcastle connect` command (CLI users connecting from outside Docker)
- `sandcastle ssh` command
- General API users who want direct SSH access

**Potential alternatives to eliminate host ports:**
1. **Mandatory Tailscale for all users** — Use Tailscale IP instead of host:port, but this removes the option for users without Tailscale
2. **SSH proxy server** — Create a sidecar container that forwards SSH connections internally (would need new port binding or proxy service)
3. **SSH on a shared port via multiplexing** — e.g., SSHD on host port 22 with subdomain/hostname-based routing (complex, non-standard)
4. **WebSocket SSH tunnel** — Provide SSH access via Traefik-based WebSocket proxy (like WeTTY but for raw SSH)
5. **API-only access** — Remove SSH entirely, force users through API/web terminal (breaks existing workflows)

---

## Architecture Summary

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    External Access                       │
│  (CLI users, admin, API consumers outside Docker)       │
└────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────┘
                 │
         ┌───────┴──────────┬─────────────────┐
         │                  │                 │
    HOST:2201-2299      Tailscale        API Endpoint
    (Direct SSH)        Bridge           /api/connect
         │                  │                 │
         └───────┬──────────┴────────────┬────┘
                 │                      │
         ┌───────▼──────────────────────▼───┐
         │  Sandbox Container               │
         │  (Sysbox, Ubuntu, SSH daemon)    │
         │                                  │
         │  Networks:                       │
         │  - None (default) ← host port    │
         │  - sandcastle-web (on-demand)    │
         │  - ts-net-{user} (if Tailscale)  │
         └──────────────────────────────────┘
                 │
         ┌───────┴──────────────┐
         │                      │
    ┌────▼────┐          ┌──────▼──────┐
    │  WeTTY  │          │   NoVNC     │
    │  Container         │  Container  │
    │ (port 3000)        │  (port 6080)│
    └─────────┘          └─────────────┘
    
    Both connected via Docker internal networking
    (no host ports needed for either)
```

---

### Bottom Line

**Short answer:** Host SSH ports are essential **only for Go CLI users and direct SSH access from outside Docker**. WeTTY and VNC work fine without them via Docker's internal networking. Eliminating them would require Tailscale to be mandatory for all users OR implementing an alternative SSH access mechanism (proxy, WebSocket tunnel, or API-only).

> AGENT

Here's what the exploration found:

**WeTTY and VNC already don't use host ports** — they connect via Docker internal networking using the container name (`ssh alice@alice-mybox` on port 22 inside `sandcastle-web` network). Host ports are only needed for **one thing**: the Go CLI (`sandcastle connect`) when Tailscale isn't enabled.

The full picture:

| What | Uses 2201-2299? | Why |
|------|:-:|---|
| WeTTY (web terminal) | No | Uses Docker DNS `{sandbox-name}:22` |
| VNC | No | Uses Docker DNS `{sandbox-name}:5900` |
| CLI `sandcastle connect` | **Yes** | Direct SSH from user's machine |
| Tailscale users | No | Already uses Tailscale IP instead |

**So the question reduces to:** how does a user without Tailscale access their sandbox via CLI?

**Three realistic options:**

1. **Make Tailscale mandatory for CLI** — simple, no proxy needed. Remove port bindings entirely, always put sandboxes on `sandcastle-web`. The web terminal still works for everyone; CLI requires Tailscale. Since this is a self-hosted platform, that's a reasonable requirement.

2. **Traefik TCP proxy via TLS/SNI** — Traefik can route TCP (SSH-over-TLS) per hostname. CLI would `ssh -o ProxyCommand='...'` through a Traefik-proxied endpoint. More complex, but keeps SSH accessible without Tailscale.

3. **Status quo with a twist** — keep port bindings but only bind to `127.0.0.1` (no public exposure). In local dev this avoids conflicts with production; in production it limits attack surface. Still needs the port range.

The cleanest path given what you already have: **option 1** — always add sandboxes to `sandcastle-web` at creation, drop `PortBindings`, and let the CLI error nicely directing users to enable Tailscale. The web terminal keeps working for everyone.

Want to go that direction?

## Complete File Contents

### 1. app/services/sandbox_manager.rb (Full File)

**Lines 1-656** - Full service class for managing sandbox lifecycle, snapshots, and Tailscale integration. Key methods:
- `create()` - Creates and starts a sandbox
- `create_container_and_start()` - Docker container creation via docker-api gem
- `ensure_image()` - Pulls or validates image availability
- `ensure_mount_dirs()` - Creates bind-mounted directories with BTRFS support
- `destroy()`, `start()`, `stop()` - Container lifecycle management
- `status()` - Returns container state info
- `create_snapshot()`, `restore()` - Multi-layer snapshot management (Docker + BTRFS)
- `connect_info()` - Returns SSH connection details with Tailscale support
- Helper methods for SSH port allocation, volume binding, container environment setup

---

### 2. vendor/sandcastle-cli/cmd/sandbox.go (Full File)

**Lines 1-497** - Go CLI commands using Cobra framework:
- `createCmd` - Creates sandboxes with flags: `--image`, `--persistent`, `--from-snapshot`, `--tailscale`, `--home`, `--data`, `--vnc-geometry`, `--vnc-depth`, `--rm`, `--no-connect`
- `listCmd` - Lists all sandboxes in tabular format
- `deleteCmd` - Destroys a sandbox
- `startCmd`, `stopCmd` - Start/stop sandboxes
- `useCmd` - Switch active server/sandbox
- `setCmd` - Toggle sandbox between temporary and kept modes
- Environment variable support: `SANDCASTLE_HOME`, `SANDCASTLE_DATA`, `SANDCASTLE_RM`

---

func (c *Client) ConnectInfo(id int) (*ConnectInfo, error) {
	var info ConnectInfo
	err := c.do("POST", fmt.Sprintf("/api/sandboxes/%d/connect", id), nil, &info)
	return &info, err
}

**Sandbox List/Get Methods (Lines 215-225):**
```go
func (c *Client) ListSandboxes() ([]Sandbox, error) {
	var sandboxes []Sandbox
	err := c.do("GET", "/api/sandboxes", nil, &sandboxes)
	return sandboxes, err
}

func (c *Client) GetSandbox(id int) (*Sandbox, error) {
	var s Sandbox
	err := c.do("GET", fmt.Sprintf("/api/sandboxes/%d", id), nil, &s)
	return &s, err
}
```

**Additional Sandbox Methods (Lines 227-253):**
```go
func (c *Client) CreateSandbox(req CreateSandboxRequest) (*Sandbox, error) {
	var s Sandbox
	err := c.do("POST", "/api/sandboxes", req, &s)
	return &s, err
}

func (c *Client) UpdateSandbox(id int, req UpdateSandboxRequest) (*Sandbox, error) {
	var s Sandbox
	err := c.do("PATCH", fmt.Sprintf("/api/sandboxes/%d", id), req, &s)
	return &s, err
}

func (c *Client) DestroySandbox(id int) error {
	return c.do("DELETE", fmt.Sprintf("/api/sandboxes/%d", id), nil, nil)
}

func (c *Client) StartSandbox(id int) (*Sandbox, error) {
	var s Sandbox
	err := c.do("POST", fmt.Sprintf("/api/sandboxes/%d/start", id), nil, &s)
	return &s, err
}

func (c *Client) StopSandbox(id int) (*Sandbox, error) {
	var s Sandbox
	err := c.do("POST", fmt.Sprintf("/api/sandboxes/%d/stop", id), nil, &s)
	return &s, err
}

### 5. db/schema.rb - Sandboxes Table Definition (Lines 81-108)

```ruby
create_table "sandboxes", force: :cascade do |t|
  t.string "container_id"
  t.datetime "created_at", null: false
  t.string "data_path"
  t.string "image", default: "ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest", null: false
  t.text "job_error"
  t.datetime "job_started_at"
  t.string "job_status"
  t.boolean "mount_home", default: false, null: false
  t.string "name", null: false
  t.boolean "persistent_volume", default: false, null: false
  t.integer "ssh_port", null: false
  t.string "status", default: "pending", null: false
  t.boolean "tailscale", default: false, null: false
  t.boolean "temporary", default: false, null: false
  t.datetime "updated_at", null: false
  t.integer "user_id", null: false
  t.integer "vnc_depth", default: 24, null: false
  t.boolean "vnc_enabled", default: true, null: false
  t.string "vnc_geometry", default: "1280x900", null: false
  t.string "volume_path"
  t.index ["container_id"], name: "index_sandboxes_on_container_id", unique: true
  t.index ["job_status"], name: "index_sandboxes_on_job_status"
  t.index ["ssh_port"], name: "index_sandboxes_on_ssh_port", unique: true, where: "((status)::text <> 'destroyed'::text)"
  t.index ["user_id", "job_status"], name: "index_sandboxes_on_user_id_and_job_status"
  t.index ["user_id", "name"], name: "index_sandboxes_on_user_id_and_name", unique: true, where: "((status)::text <> 'destroyed'::text)"
  t.index ["user_id"], name: "index_sandboxes_on_user_id"
end
```

### 7. app/controllers/api/sandboxes_controller.rb - Selected Actions (Lines 1-220)

**Connect Action (Lines 119-134):**
```ruby
def connect
  # Allow pending sandboxes to return connect info (they may still be provisioning)
  info = SandboxManager.new.connect_info(sandbox: @sandbox)
  render json: info
rescue SandboxManager::Error => e
  # If sandbox isn't ready yet, return helpful error
  if @sandbox.status == "pending"
    render json: {
      error: "Sandbox is still being provisioned",
      status: @sandbox.status,
      job_status: @sandbox.job_status
    }, status: :accepted
  else
    raise
  end
end
```

**Show Action (Lines 11-13):**
```ruby
def show
  render json: sandbox_json(@sandbox)
end
```

**Index Action (Lines 5-9):**
```ruby
def index
  authorize Sandbox
  sandboxes = policy_scope(Sandbox)
  render json: sandboxes.map { |s| sandbox_json(s) }
end
```

**Additional key methods:**
- `create()` (Lines 15-79) - Creates sandbox with optional snapshot restoration and async provisioning
- `update()` (Lines 81-84) - Updates temporary flag
- `destroy()` (Lines 86-97) - Enqueues async destroy job
- `start()`, `stop()` (Lines 99-117) - Enqueue async state change jobs

- `tailscale_connect()`, `tailscale_disconnect()` (Lines 158-166) - Tailscale connectivity
- `sandbox_json()` helper (Lines 189-217) - Serializes sandbox with job tracking and Tailscale IP

     7→  def create(user:, name:, image: DEFAULT_IMAGE, persistent: false, tailscale: false, mount_home: false, data_path: nil, temporary: false)
     8→    # Build sandbox record (not saved yet)

    25→
    26→    # Validate before doing expensive operations

    28→
    29→    # Create directories FIRST (can fail fast before saving record)
    30→    ensure_mount_dirs(user, sandbox)
    31→
    32→    # Now safe to save
    33→    sandbox.save!
    34→
    35→    # Pull image

    47→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    48→    sandbox&.update(status: "destroyed") if sandbox&.persisted?
    49→    raise Error, "Failed to create container: #{e.message}"
    50→  rescue => e
    51→    # If anything fails before save, no DB record is created

    57→  # Public method for job usage
    58→  def create_container_and_start(sandbox:, user:)
    59→    container = Docker::Container.create(
    60→      "name" => sandbox.full_name,
    61→      "Image" => sandbox.image,
    62→      "Hostname" => sandbox.full_name,
    63→      "Env" => container_env(user, sandbox),
    64→      "Labels" => { "sandcastle.sandbox" => "true" },
    65→      "HostConfig" => {
    66→        "Runtime" => container_runtime,
    67→        "PortBindings" => {
    68→          "22/tcp" => [ { "HostPort" => sandbox.ssh_port.to_s } ]
    69→        },
    70→        "Binds" => volume_binds(user, sandbox),
    71→        "RestartPolicy" => { "Name" => "unless-stopped" }
    72→      }
    73→    )
    74→
    75→    container.start
    76→    container.refresh!
    77→    unless container.json.dig("State", "Running")
    78→      state_error = container.json.dig("State", "Error").presence || container.json.dig("State", "Status")
    79→      raise Error, "Container failed to start: #{state_error}"
    80→    end
    81→    sandbox.update!(container_id: container.id, status: "running")
    82→  end
    83→
    84→  # Public method for job usage
    85→  def ensure_image(image)
    86→    Docker::Image.get(image)
    87→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
    88→    raise Error, "Snapshot image #{image} not found locally (snapshots are never pulled from a registry)" if image.start_with?("sc-snap-")
    89→    Docker::Image.create("fromImage" => image)
    90→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    91→    raise Error, "Failed to pull image #{image}: #{e.message}"
    92→  end

    94→  # Public method for job usage
    95→  def ensure_mount_dirs(user, sandbox)
    96→    # Directories bind-mounted into Sysbox containers must be world-writable
    97→    # because Sysbox maps container root to a high host UID (via /etc/subuid)
    98→    # that won't match the directory owner.
    99→
   100→    # Create BTRFS subvolume for user directory if on BTRFS
   101→    BtrfsHelper.create_user_subvolume(user.name)
   102→
   103→    if sandbox.mount_home
   104→      dir = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/home"
   105→      FileUtils.mkdir_p(dir)
   106→      FileUtils.chmod(0o777, dir)
   107→    end
   108→    if sandbox.data_path.present?
   109→      # Create BTRFS subvolume for data directory if on BTRFS
   110→      BtrfsHelper.create_user_data_subvolume(user.name, sandbox.data_path)
   111→
   112→      dir = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}".chomp("/")
   113→      FileUtils.mkdir_p(dir)
   114→      FileUtils.chmod(0o777, dir)
   115→    end

   395→			for _, alias := range aliases {

   399→				} else {

   402→			}

   405→		}

   407→		name := args[0]

   409→		// Check if name matches a server alias or URL

   412→			if err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {

   414→			}
   415→			fmt.Printf("Switched to server %s (%s)\n", name, cfg.Servers[name].URL)
   416→			return nil
   417→		}

   419→		// Try matching by URL

   438→	Long: `Toggle a sandbox between temporary (auto-remove on exit) and kept.

   440→  temp   Mark as temporary — will be removed when the CLI session exits

   442→	Args: cobra.ExactArgs(2),

   444→		client, err := api.NewClient()

   461→			temp = false
   462→		default:
   463→			return fmt.Errorf("unknown mode %q: use \"temp\" or \"keep\"", args[1])
   464→		}
   465→
   466→		sandbox, err = client.UpdateSandbox(sandbox.ID, api.UpdateSandboxRequest{Temporary: &temp})
   467→		if err != nil {
   468→			return err
   469→		}
   470→
   471→		if temp {
   472→			fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q set to temporary (will be removed on exit).\n", sandbox.Name)
   473→		} else {
   474→			fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q set to keep (will not be removed on exit).\n", sandbox.Name)
   475→		}
   476→		return nil
   477→	},
   478→}

   480→func envTruthy(key string) bool {
   481→	v := strings.ToLower(os.Getenv(key))
   482→	return v == "1" || v == "true" || v == "yes"
   483→}
   484→
   485→func findSandboxByName(client *api.Client, name string) (*api.Sandbox, error) {
   486→	sandboxes, err := client.ListSandboxes()
   487→	if err != nil {
   488→		return nil, err
   489→	}
   490→	for _, s := range sandboxes {
   491→		if s.Name == name {
   492→			return &s, nil
   493→		}
   494→	}
   495→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("sandbox %q not found", name)
   496→}

   520→  def snapshot_json(snap)
   521→    {
   522→      name: snap.name,

   535→  def connect_info(sandbox:)
   536→    host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")
   537→    user = sandbox.user.name
   538→    info = {
   539→      host: host,
   540→      port: sandbox.ssh_port,
   541→      user: user,
   542→      command: sandbox.connect_command(host: host)
   543→    }
   544→
   545→    # If Tailscale is enabled, wait for and use the bridge network IP for direct SSH access
   546→    # This works if the client is on the same Tailscale network (routes advertised by sidecar)
   547→    if sandbox.tailscale?
   548→      ts_ip = wait_for_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox)
   549→      if ts_ip.present?
   550→        info[:host] = ts_ip
   551→        info[:port] = 22
   552→        info[:command] = "ssh #{user}@#{ts_ip}"
   553→        info[:tailscale_ip] = ts_ip
   554→      end
   555→    end
   556→
   557→    info
   558→  end
   559→
   560→  private
   561→
   562→  def wait_for_tailscale_ip(sandbox:, max_attempts: 30, delay: 0.5)
   563→    # Wait for the sandbox to be provisioned (background job) and Tailscale IP to be assigned
   564→    max_attempts.times do
   565→      sandbox.reload # Refresh from DB to get latest status
   566→
   567→      # If sandbox isn't running yet, keep waiting (provision job in progress)
   568→      if sandbox.status != "running"
   569→        sleep delay
   570→        next
   571→      end
   572→
   573→      # Sandbox is running, try to get Tailscale IP
   574→      ts_ip = TailscaleManager.new.sandbox_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox)
   575→      return ts_ip if ts_ip.present?
   576→
   577→      sleep delay
   578→    end
   579→    Rails.logger.warn("Tailscale IP not available for sandbox #{sandbox.id} after #{max_attempts} attempts (status: #{sandbox.status})")
   580→    nil
   581→  end
   582→
   583→  def find_free_ssh_port
   584→    db_used = Sandbox.where.not(status: %w[destroyed stopped]).pluck(:ssh_port).compact
   585→    docker_used = docker_bound_ssh_ports
   586→    candidates = Sandbox::SSH_PORT_RANGE.to_a - (db_used + docker_used).uniq
   587→    port = candidates.find { |p| port_free?(p) }
   588→    port || raise(Error, "No SSH ports available in range #{Sandbox::SSH_PORT_RANGE}")
   589→  end
   590→
   591→  def docker_bound_ssh_ports
   592→    Docker::Container.all(all: true).flat_map do |c|
   593→      (c.info["Ports"] || []).map { |p| p["PublicPort"] }.compact
   594→    end.select { |p| Sandbox::SSH_PORT_RANGE.include?(p) }
   595→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
   596→    []
   597→  end
   598→
   599→  # Verify a port is truly free by attempting a TCP connection to the Docker host.

    24→	Args:  cobra.MaximumNArgs(1),

    99→func resolveSandboxName(args []string) string {
   100→	if len(args) > 0 {
   101→		return args[0]
   102→	}
   103→	return ""
   104→}
   105→
   106→func waitForSSH(host string, port int) error {
   107→	addr := net.JoinHostPort(host, strconv.Itoa(port))
   108→	deadline := time.Now().Add(30 * time.Second)
   109→	printed := false
   110→
   111→	if os.Getenv("VERBOSE") == "1" {
   112→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\033[2m[verbose] Waiting for SSH at %s (timeout: 30s)\033[0m\n", addr)
   113→	}
   114→
   115→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   116→		conn, err := net.DialTimeout("tcp", addr, time.Second)
   117→		if err == nil {
   118→			conn.Close()
   119→			if printed {
   120→				fmt.Println()
   121→			}
   122→			if os.Getenv("VERBOSE") == "1" {
   123→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\033[2m[verbose] SSH connection successful!\033[0m\n")
   124→			}
   125→			return nil
   126→		}
   127→		if !printed {
   128→			fmt.Printf("Waiting for SSH to be ready...")
   129→			printed = true
   130→		}
   131→		fmt.Print(".")
   132→		time.Sleep(500 * time.Millisecond)
   133→	}
   134→
   135→	fmt.Println()
   136→	return fmt.Errorf("timeout waiting for SSH at %s", addr)
   137→}
   138→
   139→func sshExec(host string, port int, user string, remoteCmd string) error {
   140→	sshArgs := []string{
   141→		"-p", strconv.Itoa(port),
   142→		"-o", "StrictHostKeyChecking=no",
   143→		"-o", "UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null",
   144→		"-o", "LogLevel=ERROR",
   145→		fmt.Sprintf("%s@%s", user, host),
   146→	}
   147→	if remoteCmd != "" {
   148→		sshArgs = append(sshArgs, "-t", remoteCmd)
   149→	}
   150→
   151→	sshPath, err := exec.LookPath("ssh")
   152→	if err != nil {
   153→		return fmt.Errorf("ssh not found: %w", err)
   154→	}
   155→
   156→	if os.Getenv("VERBOSE") == "1" {
   157→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "→ ssh %s\n", shellJoin(sshArgs))
   158→	}
   159→
   160→	proc := &os.ProcAttr{
   161→		Files: []*os.File{os.Stdin, os.Stdout, os.Stderr},
   162→	}
   163→	process, err := os.StartProcess(sshPath, append([]string{"ssh"}, sshArgs...), proc)
   164→	if err != nil {
   165→		return fmt.Errorf("starting ssh: %w", err)
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	state, err := process.Wait()
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		return err
   171→	}
   172→	if !state.Success() {
   173→		os.Exit(state.ExitCode())
   174→	}
   175→	return nil
   176→}
   177→
   178→func shellJoin(args []string) string {
   179→	quoted := make([]string, len(args))
   180→	for i, a := range args {
   181→		if strings.ContainsAny(a, " \t\n\"'\\") {
   182→			quoted[i] = fmt.Sprintf("%q", a)
   183→		} else {
   184→			quoted[i] = a
   185→		}
   186→	}
   187→	return strings.Join(quoted, " ")
   188→}
   189→

    22→

    24→  scope :running, -> { where(status: "running") }
    25→
    26→  before_validation :assign_ssh_port, on: :create
    27→
    28→  # Turbo Streams for real-time UI updates
    29→  after_create_commit :broadcast_prepend_to_dashboard
    30→  after_update_commit :broadcast_replace_to_dashboard
    31→  after_destroy_commit :broadcast_remove_from_dashboard

    49→  # Job lifecycle management
    50→  def job_in_progress?
    51→    job_status.present?
    52→  end
    53→
    54→  def job_failed?
    55→    job_error.present?
    56→  end
    57→
    58→  def start_job(status)
    59→    update!(job_status: status, job_started_at: Time.current, job_error: nil)
    60→  end
    61→
    62→  def finish_job
    63→    update!(job_status: nil, job_started_at: nil)
    64→  end
    65→

    67→    update!(job_status: nil, job_error: error_message, job_started_at: nil)
    68→  end
    69→
    70→  private
    71→
    72→  def broadcast_prepend_to_dashboard
    73→    broadcast_prepend_to(
    74→      [user, "dashboard"],
    75→      partial: "dashboard/sandbox",
    76→      locals: { sandbox: self },
    77→      target: "sandboxes"
    78→    )
    79→  end
    80→
    81→  def broadcast_replace_to_dashboard
    82→    # If sandbox is destroyed, remove it from the dashboard instead of replacing
    83→    if status == "destroyed"
    84→      broadcast_remove_to([user, "dashboard"], target: dom_id(self))
    85→    else
    86→      broadcast_replace_to(
    87→        [user, "dashboard"],
    88→        partial: "dashboard/sandbox",
    89→        locals: { sandbox: self },
    90→        target: dom_id(self)
    91→      )
    92→    end
    93→  end
    94→
    95→  def broadcast_remove_from_dashboard
    96→    broadcast_remove_to([user, "dashboard"], target: dom_id(self))
    97→  end
    98→
    99→  def assign_ssh_port
   100→    return if ssh_port.present?
   101→
   102→    used_ports = Sandbox.where.not(status: "destroyed").pluck(:ssh_port)
   103→    available = SSH_PORT_RANGE.to_a - used_ports
   104→    self.ssh_port = available.first || raise("No SSH ports available")
   105→  end
   106→end

   597→  end
   598→
   599→  # Verify a port is truly free by attempting a TCP connection to the Docker host.
   600→  # Returns true (free) if the connection is refused. Falls back to true if the
   601→  # Docker host is not reachable (e.g. production Linux without host.docker.internal).
   602→  def port_free?(port)
   603→    Timeout.timeout(0.3) { TCPSocket.new("host.docker.internal", port).close }
   604→    false # connected → port is occupied
   605→  rescue Errno::ECONNREFUSED, Timeout::Error
   606→    true  # refused/timed-out → port is free
   607→  rescue SocketError, Errno::EHOSTUNREACH, Errno::ENETUNREACH
   608→    true  # host not reachable → skip TCP check, rely on Docker API result
   609→  end
   610→
   611→  def container_env(user, sandbox)
   612→    env = [
   613→      "SANDCASTLE_USER=#{user.name}",
   614→      "SANDCASTLE_SSH_KEY=#{user.ssh_public_key}"
   615→    ]
   616→    env << "USER_EMAIL=#{user.email_address}" if user.email_address.present?
   617→    env << "USER_FULLNAME=#{user.full_name}" if user.full_name.present?
   618→    env << "SANDCASTLE_VNC_ENABLED=#{sandbox.vnc_enabled? ? '1' : '0'}"
   619→    env << "SANDCASTLE_VNC_GEOMETRY=#{sandbox.vnc_geometry}"
   620→    env << "SANDCASTLE_VNC_DEPTH=#{sandbox.vnc_depth}"
   621→    env
   622→  end

    55→  # Public method for job usage
    56→  def create_container_and_start(sandbox:, user:)
    57→    container = Docker::Container.create(
    58→      "name" => sandbox.full_name,
    59→      "Image" => sandbox.image,
    60→      "Hostname" => sandbox.full_name,
    61→      "Env" => container_env(user, sandbox),

    63→      "HostConfig" => {

    70→      }

    74→    container.refresh!

    76→      state_error = container.json.dig("State", "Error").presence || container.json.dig("State", "Status")
    77→      raise Error, "Container failed to start: #{state_error}"
    78→    end
    79→    sandbox.update!(container_id: container.id, status: "running")

    92→  # Public method for job usage
    93→  def ensure_mount_dirs(user, sandbox)
    94→    # Directories bind-mounted into Sysbox containers must be world-writable
    95→    # because Sysbox maps container root to a high host UID (via /etc/subuid)
    96→    # that won't match the directory owner.
    97→
    98→    # Create BTRFS subvolume for user directory if on BTRFS
    99→    BtrfsHelper.create_user_subvolume(user.name)
   100→
   101→    if sandbox.mount_home
   102→      dir = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/home"
   103→      FileUtils.mkdir_p(dir)
   104→      FileUtils.chmod(0o777, dir)
   105→    end
   106→    if sandbox.data_path.present?
   107→      # Create BTRFS subvolume for data directory if on BTRFS
   108→      BtrfsHelper.create_user_data_subvolume(user.name, sandbox.data_path)
   109→
   110→      dir = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}".chomp("/")
   111→      FileUtils.mkdir_p(dir)
   112→      FileUtils.chmod(0o777, dir)
   113→    end
   114→    if sandbox.persistent_volume && sandbox.volume_path
   115→      FileUtils.mkdir_p(sandbox.volume_path)
   116→      FileUtils.chmod(0o777, sandbox.volume_path)
   117→    end
   118→    # Chrome profile persistence: mount .config/google-chrome separately if not mounting full home
   119→    if user.chrome_persist_profile? && !sandbox.mount_home
   120→      dir = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/chrome-profile"
   121→      FileUtils.mkdir_p(dir)
   122→      FileUtils.chmod(0o777, dir)
   123→    end
   124→  rescue Errno::EACCES, Errno::ENOENT => e
   125→    raise Error, "Failed to create mount directories: #{e.message}"
   126→  end

   128→  def destroy(sandbox:, keep_volume: false)
   129→    begin
   130→      TerminalManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
   131→    rescue TerminalManager::Error, Docker::Error::DockerError
   132→      # best-effort terminal cleanup
   133→    end
   134→
   135→    begin
   136→      VncManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
   137→    rescue VncManager::Error, Docker::Error::DockerError
   138→      # best-effort VNC cleanup
   139→    end
   140→
   141→    RouteManager.new.remove_all_routes(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.routed?
   142→
   143→    if sandbox.tailscale?
   144→      TailscaleManager.new.disconnect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox)
   145→    end
   146→
   147→    if sandbox.container_id.present?
   148→      begin
   149→        container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   150→        container.stop(t: 5) rescue nil
   151→        container.delete(force: true)
   152→      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   153→        # Container already gone
   154→      end
   155→    end
   156→
   157→    unless keep_volume
   158→      FileUtils.rm_rf(sandbox.volume_path) if sandbox.volume_path.present?
   159→    end
   160→
   161→    sandbox.update!(status: "destroyed", container_id: nil)
   162→  end
   163→
   164→  def start(sandbox:)
   165→    raise Error, "Sandbox is destroyed" if sandbox.status == "destroyed"
   166→    return sandbox if sandbox.status == "running"
   167→
   168→    user = sandbox.user
   169→
   170→    # Port was released on stop — assign a new free port and recreate the container
   171→    new_port = find_free_ssh_port
   172→    sandbox.update!(ssh_port: new_port)
   173→
   174→    if sandbox.container_id.present?
   175→      begin
   176→        old = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   177→        old.stop(t: 5) rescue nil
   178→        old.delete(force: true)
   179→      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   180→        # already gone
   181→      end
   182→    end
   183→
   184→    create_container_and_start(sandbox: sandbox, user: user)
   185→
   186→    RouteManager.new.reconnect_routes(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.routed?
   187→
   188→    sandbox
   189→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   190→    sandbox.update!(status: "destroyed", container_id: nil)
   191→    raise Error, "Container not found — sandbox must be recreated"
   192→  end
   193→
   194→  def stop(sandbox:)
   195→    return sandbox if sandbox.status == "stopped"
   196→
   197→    begin
   198→      TerminalManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
   199→    rescue TerminalManager::Error, Docker::Error::DockerError
   200→      # best-effort terminal cleanup
   201→    end
   202→
   203→    RouteManager.new.suspend_routes(sandbox: sandbox) if sandbox.routed?
   204→
   205→    if sandbox.container_id.present?
   206→      begin
   207→        container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   208→        container.stop(t: 10)
   209→      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   210→        # already gone
   211→      end
   212→    end
   213→
   214→    sandbox.update!(status: "stopped", ssh_port: nil)
   215→    sandbox
   216→  end

   218→  def status(sandbox:)
   219→    return { state: "destroyed" } if sandbox.container_id.blank?
   220→
   221→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   222→    info = container.json
   223→    {
   224→      state: info.dig("State", "Status"),
   225→      running: info.dig("State", "Running"),
   226→      started_at: info.dig("State", "StartedAt"),
   227→      pid: info.dig("State", "Pid")
   228→    }
   229→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   230→    { state: "not_found" }
   231→  end
   232→
   233→  # Create a composite snapshot (Docker image + optional BTRFS layers).
   234→  # Returns a Snapshot ActiveRecord object.
   235→  #
   236→  # layers: array of "container", "home", "data", "workspace"
   237→  #   nil means "all available" based on sandbox config
   238→  def create_snapshot(sandbox:, name:, label: nil, layers: nil, data_subdir: nil)
   239→    raise Error, "Sandbox has no running container" if sandbox.container_id.blank?
   240→
   241→    user = sandbox.user
   242→    name = name.presence || Date.today.iso8601
   243→    requested_layers = layers&.map(&:to_s) || %w[container home data workspace]
   244→
   245→    snap = Snapshot.new(
   246→      user: user,
   247→      name: name,
   248→      label: label,
   249→      source_sandbox: sandbox.name,
   250→      data_subdir: data_subdir
   251→    )
   252→
   253→    # ── Container layer ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   254→    if requested_layers.include?("container")
   255→      repo = "sc-snap-#{user.name}"
   256→      container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   257→      image = container.commit(
   258→        repo: repo,
   259→        tag: name,
   260→        comment: "sandbox:#{sandbox.name}"
   261→      )
   262→      snap.docker_image = "#{repo}:#{name}"
   263→      snap.docker_size  = image.info["Size"]
   264→    end
   265→
   266→    # ── Home layer (BTRFS only) ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   267→    if requested_layers.include?("home") && sandbox.mount_home? && BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
   268→      home_src  = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/home"
   269→      home_dest = "#{DATA_DIR}/snapshots/#{user.name}/#{name}/home"
   270→      if Dir.exist?(home_src)
   271→        BtrfsHelper.snapshot_subvolume(home_src, home_dest)
   272→        snap.home_snapshot = home_dest
   273→        snap.home_size     = BtrfsHelper.subvolume_size(home_dest)
   274→      end
   275→    end
   276→
   277→    # ── Data layer (BTRFS only) ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   278→    if requested_layers.include?("data") && sandbox.data_path.present? && BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
   279→      if data_subdir.present?
   280→        data_src  = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}/#{data_subdir}".chomp("/")
   281→      else
   282→        data_src  = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}".chomp("/")
   283→      end
   284→      data_dest = "#{DATA_DIR}/snapshots/#{user.name}/#{name}/data"
   285→      if Dir.exist?(data_src)
   286→        BtrfsHelper.snapshot_subvolume(data_src, data_dest)
   287→        snap.data_snapshot = data_dest
   288→        snap.data_size     = BtrfsHelper.subvolume_size(data_dest)
   289→      end
   290→    end
   291→
   292→    # ── Workspace layer (BTRFS only) ─────────────────────────────────────────
   293→    if requested_layers.include?("workspace") && sandbox.persistent_volume? && sandbox.volume_path.present? && BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
   294→      workspace_src  = sandbox.volume_path
   295→      workspace_dest = "#{DATA_DIR}/snapshots/#{user.name}/#{name}/workspace"
   296→      if Dir.exist?(workspace_src)
   297→        BtrfsHelper.snapshot_subvolume(workspace_src, workspace_dest)
   298→        # Store workspace alongside data_snapshot when no data layer taken
   299→        snap.data_snapshot ||= workspace_dest
   300→        snap.data_size     = BtrfsHelper.subvolume_size(workspace_dest)
   301→      end
   302→    end
   303→
   304→    snap.save!
   305→    snap
   306→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
   307→    raise Error, "Failed to create snapshot: #{e.message}"
   308→  rescue BtrfsHelper::Error => e
   309→    raise Error, "Failed to snapshot filesystem layer: #{e.message}"
   310→  end
   311→
   312→  # Legacy alias kept for backward compatibility (used by existing API endpoint).
   313→  def snapshot(sandbox:, name: nil, **opts)
   314→    snap = create_snapshot(sandbox: sandbox, name: name, layers: %w[container])
   315→    {
   316→      name: snap.name,
   317→      image: snap.docker_image,
   318→      sandbox: snap.source_sandbox,
   319→      created_at: snap.created_at
   320→    }
   321→  end
   322→
   323→  def list_snapshots(user:)
   324→    import_legacy_snapshots(user)
   325→    Snapshot.where(user: user).order(created_at: :desc).map { |s| snapshot_json(s) }
   326→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
   327→    raise Error, "Failed to list snapshots: #{e.message}"
   328→  end
   329→
   330→  def find_snapshot(user:, name:)
   331→    import_legacy_snapshots(user)
   332→    Snapshot.find_by!(user: user, name: name)
   333→  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound
   334→    raise Error, "Snapshot '#{name}' not found"
   335→  end
   336→
   337→  def destroy_snapshot(user:, name:)
   338→    snap = Snapshot.find_by(user: user, name: name)
   339→
   340→    if snap
   341→      # Remove Docker image
   342→      if snap.docker_image.present?
   343→        begin
   344→          Docker::Image.get(snap.docker_image).remove
   345→        rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   346→          # Already gone
   347→        rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
   348→          raise Error, "Failed to remove Docker image: #{e.message}"
   349→        end
   350→      end
   351→
   352→      # Remove BTRFS snapshots
   353→      if snap.home_snapshot.present?
   354→        begin
   355→          BtrfsHelper.delete_snapshot(snap.home_snapshot)
   356→        rescue BtrfsHelper::Error => e
   357→          Rails.logger.warn("Could not delete home snapshot #{snap.home_snapshot}: #{e.message}")
   358→        end
   359→      end
   360→
   361→      if snap.data_snapshot.present?
   362→        begin
   363→          BtrfsHelper.delete_snapshot(snap.data_snapshot)
   364→        rescue BtrfsHelper::Error => e
   365→          Rails.logger.warn("Could not delete data snapshot #{snap.data_snapshot}: #{e.message}")
   366→        end
   367→      end
   368→
   369→      snap.destroy!
   370→    else
   371→      # Legacy: try to find and remove Docker image directly
   372→      image_ref = "sc-snap-#{user.name}:#{name}"
   373→      begin
   374→        Docker::Image.get(image_ref).remove
   375→      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   376→        raise Error, "Snapshot '#{name}' not found"
   377→      rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
   378→        raise Error, "Failed to destroy snapshot: #{e.message}"
   379→      end
   380→    end
   381→  end
   382→
   383→  def restore(sandbox:, snapshot_name:, layers: nil)
   384→    user = sandbox.user
   385→    was_tailscale = sandbox.tailscale?
   386→
   387→    snap = Snapshot.find_by(user: user, name: snapshot_name)
   388→    requested_layers = layers&.map(&:to_s)
   389→
   390→    # Determine the Docker image to use
   391→    if snap&.docker_image.present?
   392→      image_ref = snap.docker_image
   393→    else
   394→      # Fall back to legacy naming
   395→      image_ref = "sc-snap-#{user.name}:#{snapshot_name}"
   396→    end
   397→
   398→    restore_container = requested_layers.nil? || requested_layers.include?("container")
   399→    restore_home      = snap&.home_snapshot.present? && (requested_layers.nil? || requested_layers.include?("home"))
   400→    restore_data      = snap&.data_snapshot.present? && (requested_layers.nil? || requested_layers.include?("data"))
   401→
   402→    # Validate the Docker image exists if we need it
   403→    if restore_container
   404→      begin
   405→        Docker::Image.get(image_ref)
   406→      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   407→        raise Error, "Snapshot '#{snapshot_name}' not found"
   408→      end
   409→    end
   410→
   411→    begin
   412→      TerminalManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
   413→    rescue TerminalManager::Error, Docker::Error::DockerError
   414→      # best-effort terminal cleanup
   415→    end
   416→
   417→    begin
   418→      VncManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
   419→    rescue VncManager::Error, Docker::Error::DockerError
   420→      # best-effort VNC cleanup
   421→    end
   422→
   423→    if sandbox.tailscale?
   424→      TailscaleManager.new.disconnect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox)
   425→    end
   426→
   427→    if sandbox.container_id.present?
   428→      begin
   429→        old_container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   430→        old_container.stop(t: 5) rescue nil
   431→        old_container.delete(force: true)
   432→      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   433→        # Already gone
   434→      end
   435→    end
   436→
   437→    # ── Restore home directory ────────────────────────────────────────────────
   438→    if restore_home
   439→      home_target = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/home"
   440→      begin
   441→        BtrfsHelper.restore_subvolume(snap.home_snapshot, home_target)
   442→      rescue BtrfsHelper::Error => e
   443→        Rails.logger.warn("Could not restore home snapshot: #{e.message}")
   444→      end
   445→    end
   446→
   447→    # ── Restore data directory ────────────────────────────────────────────────
   448→    if restore_data
   449→      if snap.data_subdir.present?
   450→        data_target = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}/#{snap.data_subdir}".chomp("/")
   451→      else
   452→        data_target = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}".chomp("/")
   453→      end
   454→      begin
   455→        BtrfsHelper.restore_subvolume(snap.data_snapshot, data_target)
   456→      rescue BtrfsHelper::Error => e
   457→        Rails.logger.warn("Could not restore data snapshot: #{e.message}")
   458→      end
   459→    end
   460→
   461→    # ── Recreate container ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   462→    final_image = restore_container ? image_ref : sandbox.image
   463→
   464→    container = Docker::Container.create(
   465→      "name" => sandbox.full_name,
   466→      "Image" => final_image,
   467→      "Hostname" => sandbox.full_name,
   468→      "Env" => container_env(user, sandbox),
   469→      "Labels" => { "sandcastle.sandbox" => "true" },
   470→      "HostConfig" => {
   471→        "Runtime" => container_runtime,
   472→        "PortBindings" => {
   473→          "22/tcp" => [ { "HostPort" => sandbox.ssh_port.to_s } ]
   474→        },
   475→        "Binds" => volume_binds(user, sandbox),
   476→        "RestartPolicy" => { "Name" => "unless-stopped" }
   477→      }
   478→    )
   479→
   480→    container.start
   481→    sandbox.update!(container_id: container.id, image: final_image, status: "running")
   482→
   483→    if was_tailscale && user.tailscale_enabled?
   484→      TailscaleManager.new.connect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox)
   485→    end
   486→
   487→    sandbox
   488→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
   489→    raise Error, "Failed to restore snapshot: #{e.message}"
   490→  end
   491→
   492→  # Import legacy Docker-only snapshots as DB records (idempotent).
   493→  def import_legacy_snapshots(user)
   494→    repo_prefix = "sc-snap-#{user.name}"
   495→
   496→    Docker::Image.all.each do |img|
   497→      repo_tags = img.info["RepoTags"] || []
   498→      repo_tags.each do |tag|
   499→        repo, tag_name = tag.split(":")
   500→        next unless repo == repo_prefix
   501→        next if tag_name.blank?
   502→        next if Snapshot.exists?(user: user, name: tag_name)
   503→
   504→        Snapshot.create!(
   505→          user: user,
   506→          name: tag_name,
   507→          docker_image: tag,
   508→          docker_size: img.info["Size"],
   509→          source_sandbox: img.info.dig("Comment")&.delete_prefix("sandbox:"),
   510→          created_at: Time.at(img.info["Created"] || Time.current.to_i)
   511→        )
   512→      end
   513→    end
   514→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError, ActiveRecord::RecordInvalid => e
   515→    Rails.logger.warn("Legacy snapshot import failed: #{e.message}")
   516→  end
   517→
   518→  def snapshot_json(snap)
   519→    {
   520→      name: snap.name,
   521→      label: snap.label,
   522→      source_sandbox: snap.source_sandbox,
   523→      layers: snap.layers,
   524→      docker_image: snap.docker_image,
   525→      docker_size: snap.docker_size,
   526→      home_size: snap.home_size,
   527→      data_size: snap.data_size,
   528→      total_size: snap.total_size,
   529→      created_at: snap.created_at
   530→    }
   531→  end
   532→
   533→  def connect_info(sandbox:)
   534→    host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")
   535→    user = sandbox.user.name
   536→    info = {
   537→      host: host,
   538→      port: sandbox.ssh_port,
   539→      user: user,
   540→      command: sandbox.connect_command(host: host)
   541→    }
   542→
   543→    # If Tailscale is enabled, wait for and use the bridge network IP for direct SSH access
   544→    # This works if the client is on the same Tailscale network (routes advertised by sidecar)
   545→    if sandbox.tailscale?
   546→      ts_ip = wait_for_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox)
   547→      if ts_ip.present?
   548→        info[:host] = ts_ip
   549→        info[:port] = 22
   550→        info[:command] = "ssh #{user}@#{ts_ip}"
   551→        info[:tailscale_ip] = ts_ip
   552→      end
   553→    end
   554→
   555→    info
   556→  end
   557→
   558→  private
   559→
   560→  def wait_for_tailscale_ip(sandbox:, max_attempts: 30, delay: 0.5)
   561→    # Wait for the sandbox to be provisioned (background job) and Tailscale IP to be assigned
   562→    max_attempts.times do
   563→      sandbox.reload # Refresh from DB to get latest status
   564→
   565→      # If sandbox isn't running yet, keep waiting (provision job in progress)
   566→      if sandbox.status != "running"
   567→        sleep delay
   568→        next
   569→      end
   570→
   571→      # Sandbox is running, try to get Tailscale IP
   572→      ts_ip = TailscaleManager.new.sandbox_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox)
   573→      return ts_ip if ts_ip.present?
   574→
   575→      sleep delay
   576→    end
   577→    Rails.logger.warn("Tailscale IP not available for sandbox #{sandbox.id} after #{max_attempts} attempts (status: #{sandbox.status})")
   578→    nil
   579→  end
   580→
   581→  def find_free_ssh_port
   582→    db_used = Sandbox.where.not(status: %w[destroyed stopped]).pluck(:ssh_port).compact
   583→    docker_used = docker_bound_ssh_ports
   584→    candidates = Sandbox::SSH_PORT_RANGE.to_a - (db_used + docker_used).uniq
   585→    port = candidates.find { |p| port_free?(p) }
   586→    port || raise(Error, "No SSH ports available in range #{Sandbox::SSH_PORT_RANGE}")
   587→  end
   588→
   589→  def docker_bound_ssh_ports
   590→    Docker::Container.all(all: true).flat_map do |c|
   591→      (c.info["Ports"] || []).map { |p| p["PublicPort"] }.compact
   592→    end.select { |p| Sandbox::SSH_PORT_RANGE.include?(p) }
   593→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
   594→    []
   595→  end
   596→
   597→  # Verify a port is truly free by attempting a TCP connection to the Docker host.
   598→  # Returns true (free) if the connection is refused. Falls back to true if the
   599→  # Docker host is not reachable (e.g. production Linux without host.docker.internal).
   600→  def port_free?(port)
   601→    Timeout.timeout(0.3) { TCPSocket.new("host.docker.internal", port).close }
   602→    false # connected → port is occupied
   603→  rescue Errno::ECONNREFUSED, Timeout::Error
   604→    true  # refused/timed-out → port is free
   605→  rescue SocketError, Errno::EHOSTUNREACH, Errno::ENETUNREACH
   606→    true  # host not reachable → skip TCP check, rely on Docker API result
   607→  end
   608→
   609→  def container_env(user, sandbox)
   610→    env = [
   611→      "SANDCASTLE_USER=#{user.name}",
   612→      "SANDCASTLE_SSH_KEY=#{user.ssh_public_key}"
   613→    ]
   614→    env << "USER_EMAIL=#{user.email_address}" if user.email_address.present?
   615→    env << "USER_FULLNAME=#{user.full_name}" if user.full_name.present?
   616→    env << "SANDCASTLE_VNC_ENABLED=#{sandbox.vnc_enabled? ? '1' : '0'}"
   617→    env << "SANDCASTLE_VNC_GEOMETRY=#{sandbox.vnc_geometry}"
   618→    env << "SANDCASTLE_VNC_DEPTH=#{sandbox.vnc_depth}"
   619→    env
   620→  end
   621→
   622→  def container_runtime
   623→    @container_runtime ||= begin
   624→      runtimes = Docker.info["Runtimes"] || {}
   625→      if runtimes.key?("sysbox-runc")
   626→        "sysbox-runc"
   627→      else
   628→        Rails.logger.warn("SandboxManager: sysbox-runc not available, falling back to runc (Docker-in-Docker will not work inside sandboxes)")
   629→        "runc"
   630→      end
   631→    end
   632→  end
   633→
   634→  def volume_binds(user, sandbox)
   635→    binds = []
   636→    if sandbox.mount_home
   637→      binds << "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/home:/home/#{user.name}"
   638→    end
   639→    if sandbox.persistent_volume && sandbox.volume_path
   640→      binds << "#{sandbox.volume_path}:/workspace"
   641→    end
   642→    if sandbox.data_path.present?
   643→      host_path = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}".chomp("/")
   644→      binds << "#{host_path}:/data"
   645→    end
   646→    # Chrome profile persistence: mount separately if not mounting full home
   647→    if user.chrome_persist_profile? && !sandbox.mount_home
   648→      host_path = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/chrome-profile"
   649→      binds << "#{host_path}:/home/#{user.name}/.config/google-chrome"
   650→    end
   651→    binds
   652→  end
   653→end
   654→

   181→        raise Pundit::NotAuthorizedError, "You don't have access to this sandbox"

   184→        Rails.logger.error("Sandbox #{params[:id]} exists (status: #{sandbox.status}, user: #{sandbox.user_id}) but not in policy_scope for user #{current_user.id} (admin: #{current_user.admin?})")
   185→        raise ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound, "Sandbox with ID #{params[:id]} is not accessible (status: #{sandbox.status})"
   186→      end
   187→    end

    57→var createCmd = &cobra.Command{
    58→	Use:   "create [name]",
    59→	Short: "Create a new sandbox",
    60→	Long: `Create a new sandbox.
    61→
    62→If no name is provided, creates a temporary sandbox with an auto-generated name like "temp-<timestamp>".
    63→
    64→Environment variables can set defaults for commonly used flags:
    65→  SANDCASTLE_HOME=1    equivalent to --home

    67→  SANDCASTLE_RM=1      equivalent to --rm
    68→
    69→Flags explicitly passed on the command line take precedence over environment variables.`,
    70→	Args: cobra.MaximumNArgs(1),
    71→	PreRun: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) {
    72→		if !cmd.Flags().Changed("home") && envTruthy("SANDCASTLE_HOME") {
    73→			sandboxHome = true
    74→		}
    75→		if !cmd.Flags().Changed("rm") && envTruthy("SANDCASTLE_RM") {
    76→			sandboxRemove = true
    77→		}
    78→		if !cmd.Flags().Changed("data") {
    79→			if v := os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_DATA"); v != "" {
    80→				if v == "1" || v == "true" {
    81→					v = "."
    82→				}
    83→				sandboxData = v
    84→			}
    85→		}
    86→	},
    87→	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
    88→		client, err := api.NewClient()
    89→		if err != nil {
    90→			return err
    91→		}
    92→		printServer(client)
    93→
    94→		// Auto-generate name if not provided
    95→		var name string
    96→		autoGenerated := false
    97→		if len(args) == 0 {
    98→			name = fmt.Sprintf("temp-%d", time.Now().Unix())
    99→			autoGenerated = true
   100→			// Auto-generated sandboxes are temporary by default
   101→			if !cmd.Flags().Changed("rm") {
   102→				sandboxRemove = true
   103→			}
   104→		} else {
   105→			name = args[0]
   106→		}
   107→
   108→		// Resolve snapshot flags: --from-snapshot takes precedence over --snapshot
   109→		fromSnap := sandboxFromSnapshot
   110→		if fromSnap == "" {
   111→			fromSnap = sandboxSnapshot
   112→		}
   113→
   114→		var restoreLayers []string
   115→		if sandboxRestoreLayers != "" {
   116→			for _, l := range strings.Split(sandboxRestoreLayers, ",") {
   117→				restoreLayers = append(restoreLayers, strings.TrimSpace(l))
   118→			}
   119→		}
   120→
   121→		sandbox, err := client.CreateSandbox(api.CreateSandboxRequest{
   122→			Name:          name,
   123→			Image:         sandboxImage,
   124→			Persistent:    sandboxPersistent,
   125→			FromSnapshot:  fromSnap,
   126→			RestoreLayers: restoreLayers,
   127→			Tailscale:     sandboxTailscale,
   128→			MountHome:     sandboxHome,
   129→			DataPath:      sandboxData,
   130→			Temporary:     sandboxRemove,
   131→			VNCEnabled:    !sandboxNoVNC,
   132→			VNCGeometry:   sandboxVNCGeometry,
   133→			VNCDepth:      sandboxVNCDepth,
   134→		})
   135→		if err != nil {
   136→			return err
   137→		}
   138→
   139→		if autoGenerated {
   140→			fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q created (auto-generated name).\n", sandbox.Name)
   141→		} else {
   142→			fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q created.\n", sandbox.Name)
   143→		}
   144→
   145→		// Print active options (use local flags — they reflect what was actually requested)
   146→		if sandboxHome || sandboxData != "" || sandboxPersistent || sandbox.Tailscale || sandboxRemove || fromSnap != "" || sandboxNoVNC || sandboxVNCGeometry != "" || sandboxVNCDepth != 0 {
   147→			if sandboxHome {
   148→				fmt.Println("  Home:      mounted (~/ persisted)")
   149→			}
   150→			if sandboxData != "" {
   151→				label := sandboxData
   152→				if label == "." {
   153→					label = "user data root"
   154→				}
   155→				fmt.Printf("  Data:      mounted (%s → /data)\n", label)
   156→			}
   157→			if sandboxPersistent {
   158→				fmt.Println("  Volume:    persistent (/workspace)")
   159→			}
   160→			if sandbox.Tailscale {
   161→				fmt.Println("  Tailscale: enabled")
   162→			}
   163→			if sandboxRemove {
   164→				fmt.Println("  Cleanup:   auto-remove on exit")
   165→			}
   166→			if fromSnap != "" {
   167→				fmt.Printf("  Snapshot:  restored from %q\n", fromSnap)
   168→			}
   169→			if sandboxNoVNC {
   170→				fmt.Println("  VNC:       disabled")
   171→			} else if sandboxVNCGeometry != "" || sandboxVNCDepth != 0 {
   172→				geom := sandbox.VNCGeometry
   173→				if geom == "" {
   174→					geom = "1280x900"
   175→				}
   176→				depth := sandbox.VNCDepth
   177→				if depth == 0 {
   178→					depth = 24
   179→				}
   180→				fmt.Printf("  VNC:       %s @ %d-bit\n", geom, depth)
   181→			}
   182→		}
   183→
   184→		if sandboxNoConnect {
   185→			return nil
   186→		}
   187→
   188→		info, err := client.ConnectInfo(sandbox.ID)
   189→		if err != nil {
   190→			return err
   191→		}
   192→
   193→		if os.Getenv("VERBOSE") == "1" {
   194→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\033[2m[verbose] Connection info: host=%s port=%d user=%s\033[0m\n", info.Host, info.Port, info.User)
   195→			if info.TailscaleIP != "" {
   196→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\033[2m[verbose] Tailscale IP: %s\033[0m\n", info.TailscaleIP)
   197→			}
   198→		}
   199→
   200→		if err := waitForSSH(info.Host, info.Port); err != nil {
   201→			return err
   202→		}
   203→
   204→		sshErr := sshExec(info.Host, info.Port, info.User, "tmux new-session -A -s main")
   205→
   206→		if sandboxRemove {
   207→			// Re-fetch to check if user toggled to "keep" during the session
   208→			current, fetchErr := client.GetSandbox(sandbox.ID)
   209→			if fetchErr == nil && !current.Temporary {
   210→				fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q was set to keep — skipping removal.\n", sandbox.Name)
   211→			} else {
   212→				fmt.Printf("Removing sandbox %q...\n", sandbox.Name)
   213→				if err := client.DestroySandbox(sandbox.ID); err != nil {
   214→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to delete sandbox: %v\n", err)
   215→				} else {
   216→					fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q deleted.\n", sandbox.Name)
   217→				}
   218→			}
   219→		}
   220→
   221→		return sshErr
   222→	},
   223→}
