   225→var listCmd = &cobra.Command{
   226→	Use:     "list",
   227→	Aliases: []string{"ls"},
   228→	Short:   "List all sandboxes",
   229→	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   230→		client, err := api.NewClient()
   231→		if err != nil {
   232→			return err
   233→		}
   234→		printServer(client)
   235→
   236→		sandboxes, err := client.ListSandboxes()
   237→		if err != nil {
   238→			return err
   239→		}
   240→
   241→		if len(sandboxes) == 0 {
   242→			fmt.Println("No sandboxes.")
   243→			return nil
   244→		}
   245→
   246→		hasRoute := false
   247→		for _, s := range sandboxes {
   248→			if len(s.Routes) > 0 {
   249→				hasRoute = true
   250→				break
   251→			}
   252→		}
   253→
   254→		w := tabwriter.NewWriter(os.Stdout, 0, 0, 2, ' ', 0)
   255→		if hasRoute {
   256→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "NAME\tSTATUS\tPORT\tROUTE\tTAILSCALE IP\tIMAGE")
   257→		} else {
   258→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "NAME\tSTATUS\tPORT\tTAILSCALE IP\tIMAGE")
   259→		}
   260→		for _, s := range sandboxes {
   261→			name := s.Name
   262→			if s.Temporary {
   263→				name += " (temp)"
   264→			}
   265→			tsIP := ""
   266→			if s.TailscaleIP != "" {
   267→				tsIP = s.TailscaleIP
   268→			}
   269→			if hasRoute {
   270→				route := ""
   271→				if len(s.Routes) > 0 {
   272→					parts := make([]string, len(s.Routes))
   273→					for i, r := range s.Routes {
   274→						parts[i] = fmt.Sprintf("%s (:%d)", r.URL, r.Port)
   275→					}
   276→					route = strings.Join(parts, ", ")
   277→				}
   278→				fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\t%s\t%d\t%s\t%s\t%s\n", name, s.Status, s.SSHPort, route, tsIP, s.Image)
   279→			} else {
   280→				fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\t%s\t%d\t%s\t%s\n", name, s.Status, s.SSHPort, tsIP, s.Image)
   281→			}
   282→		}
   283→		w.Flush()
   284→		return nil
   285→	},
   286→}

   365→var useCmd = &cobra.Command{
   366→	Use:   "use [name]",
   367→	Short: "Show or set active server/sandbox",
   368→	Long: `Without arguments, shows the current server and active sandbox.
   369→With an argument, switches the active server or sandbox.
   370→
   371→Examples:
   372→  sandcastle use                  # Show current server and sandbox
   373→  sandcastle use my-sandbox       # Set active sandbox
   374→  sandcastle use prod             # Switch to server "prod" (if configured)`,
   375→	Args: cobra.MaximumNArgs(1),
   376→	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   377→		cfg, err := config.Load()
   378→		if err != nil {
   379→			return err
   380→		}
   381→
   382→		// No args: list all servers, highlight active
   383→		if len(args) == 0 {
   384→			if len(cfg.Servers) == 0 {
   385→				fmt.Println("No servers configured — run: sandcastle login <url>")
   386→				return nil
   387→			}
   388→
   389→			aliases := make([]string, 0, len(cfg.Servers))
   390→			for alias := range cfg.Servers {
   391→				aliases = append(aliases, alias)
   392→			}
   393→			sort.Strings(aliases)
   394→
   395→			for _, alias := range aliases {
   396→				srv := cfg.Servers[alias]
   397→				if alias == cfg.CurrentServer {
   398→					fmt.Printf("  \033[1m* %-16s\033[0m %s\n", alias, srv.URL)
   399→				} else {
   400→					fmt.Printf("    %-16s %s\n", alias, srv.URL)
   401→				}
   402→			}
   403→
   404→			return nil
   405→		}
   406→
   407→		name := args[0]
   408→
   409→		// Check if name matches a server alias or URL
   410→		if _, ok := cfg.Servers[name]; ok {
   411→			cfg.CurrentServer = name
   412→			if err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {
   413→				return err
   414→			}
   415→			fmt.Printf("Switched to server %s (%s)\n", name, cfg.Servers[name].URL)
   416→			return nil
   417→		}
   418→
   419→		// Try matching by URL
   420→		for alias, srv := range cfg.Servers {
   421→			if srv.URL == strings.TrimRight(name, "/") {
   422→				cfg.CurrentServer = alias
   423→				if err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {
   424→					return err
   425→				}
   426→				fmt.Printf("Switched to server %s (%s)\n", alias, srv.URL)
   427→				return nil
   428→			}
   429→		}
   430→
   431→		return fmt.Errorf("server %q not found — run: sandcastle use", name)
   432→	},
   433→}
   434→
   435→var setCmd = &cobra.Command{
   436→	Use:   "set <name> <temp|keep>",
   437→	Short: "Toggle sandbox between temporary and kept",
   438→	Long: `Toggle a sandbox between temporary (auto-remove on exit) and kept.
   439→
   440→  temp   Mark as temporary — will be removed when the CLI session exits
   441→  keep   Mark as kept — will not be auto-removed`,
   442→	Args: cobra.ExactArgs(2),
   443→	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   444→		client, err := api.NewClient()
   445→		if err != nil {
   446→			return err
   447→		}
   448→		printServer(client)
   449→
   450→		sandbox, err := findSandboxByName(client, args[0])
   451→		if err != nil {
   452→			return err
   453→		}
   454→
   455→		mode := strings.ToLower(args[1])
   456→		var temp bool
   457→		switch mode {
   458→		case "temp":
   459→			temp = true
   460→		case "keep":
   461→			temp = false
   462→		default:
   463→			return fmt.Errorf("unknown mode %q: use \"temp\" or \"keep\"", args[1])
   464→		}
   465→
   466→		sandbox, err = client.UpdateSandbox(sandbox.ID, api.UpdateSandboxRequest{Temporary: &temp})
   467→		if err != nil {
   468→			return err
   469→		}
   470→
   471→		if temp {
   472→			fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q set to temporary (will be removed on exit).\n", sandbox.Name)
   473→		} else {
   474→			fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q set to keep (will not be removed on exit).\n", sandbox.Name)
   475→		}
   476→		return nil
   477→	},
   478→}
   479→
   480→func envTruthy(key string) bool {
   481→	v := strings.ToLower(os.Getenv(key))
   482→	return v == "1" || v == "true" || v == "yes"
   483→}
   484→
   485→func findSandboxByName(client *api.Client, name string) (*api.Sandbox, error) {
   486→	sandboxes, err := client.ListSandboxes()
   487→	if err != nil {
   488→		return nil, err
   489→	}
   490→	for _, s := range sandboxes {
   491→		if s.Name == name {
   492→			return &s, nil
   493→		}
   494→	}
   495→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("sandbox %q not found", name)
   496→}

    24→	Args:  cobra.MaximumNArgs(1),
    25→	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
    26→		name := resolveSandboxName(args)

    29→		}
    30→

    49→		}

    54→		}

    58→		}
    59→

    62→	},
    63→}

    98→
    99→func resolveSandboxName(args []string) string {
   100→	if len(args) > 0 {
   101→		return args[0]
   102→	}
   103→	return ""
   104→}
   105→
   106→func waitForSSH(host string, port int) error {
   107→	addr := net.JoinHostPort(host, strconv.Itoa(port))
   108→	deadline := time.Now().Add(30 * time.Second)
   109→	printed := false
   110→
   111→	if os.Getenv("VERBOSE") == "1" {
   112→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\033[2m[verbose] Waiting for SSH at %s (timeout: 30s)\033[0m\n", addr)
   113→	}
   114→
   115→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   116→		conn, err := net.DialTimeout("tcp", addr, time.Second)
   117→		if err == nil {
   118→			conn.Close()
   119→			if printed {
   120→				fmt.Println()
   121→			}
   122→			if os.Getenv("VERBOSE") == "1" {
   123→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\033[2m[verbose] SSH connection successful!\033[0m\n")
   124→			}
   125→			return nil
   126→		}
   127→		if !printed {
   128→			fmt.Printf("Waiting for SSH to be ready...")
   129→			printed = true
   130→		}
   131→		fmt.Print(".")
   132→		time.Sleep(500 * time.Millisecond)
   133→	}
   134→
   135→	fmt.Println()
   136→	return fmt.Errorf("timeout waiting for SSH at %s", addr)
   137→}
   138→
   139→func sshExec(host string, port int, user string, remoteCmd string) error {
   140→	sshArgs := []string{
   141→		"-p", strconv.Itoa(port),
   142→		"-o", "StrictHostKeyChecking=no",
   143→		"-o", "UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null",
   144→		"-o", "LogLevel=ERROR",
   145→		fmt.Sprintf("%s@%s", user, host),
   146→	}
   147→	if remoteCmd != "" {
   148→		sshArgs = append(sshArgs, "-t", remoteCmd)
   149→	}
   150→
   151→	sshPath, err := exec.LookPath("ssh")
   152→	if err != nil {
   153→		return fmt.Errorf("ssh not found: %w", err)
   154→	}
   155→
   156→	if os.Getenv("VERBOSE") == "1" {
   157→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "→ ssh %s\n", shellJoin(sshArgs))
   158→	}
   159→
   160→	proc := &os.ProcAttr{
   161→		Files: []*os.File{os.Stdin, os.Stdout, os.Stderr},
   162→	}
   163→	process, err := os.StartProcess(sshPath, append([]string{"ssh"}, sshArgs...), proc)
   164→	if err != nil {
   165→		return fmt.Errorf("starting ssh: %w", err)
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	state, err := process.Wait()
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		return err
   171→	}
   172→	if !state.Success() {
   173→		os.Exit(state.ExitCode())
   174→	}
   175→	return nil
   176→}
   177→
   178→func shellJoin(args []string) string {
   179→	quoted := make([]string, len(args))
   180→	for i, a := range args {
   181→		if strings.ContainsAny(a, " \t\n\"'\\") {
   182→			quoted[i] = fmt.Sprintf("%q", a)
   183→		} else {
   184→			quoted[i] = a
   185→		}
   186→	}
   187→	return strings.Join(quoted, " ")
   188→}
   189→

Enable Tailscale on your account and connect this sandbox to your tailnet.\"\n    end\n\n    ts_ip = wait_for_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox)\n    raise Error, \"Tailscale IP not available — is the Tailscale sidecar running?\" unless ts_ip.present?\n\n    {\n      host: ts_ip,\n      port: 22,\n      user: user,\n      command: \"ssh #{user}@#{ts_ip}\",\n      tailscale_ip: ts_ip\n    }\n  end"

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/sandbox_manager.rb",
  "old_string": "  def find_free_ssh_port\n    db_used = Sandbox.where.not(status: %w[destroyed stopped]).pluck(:ssh_port).compact\n    docker_used = docker_bound_ssh_ports\n    candidates = Sandbox::SSH_PORT_RANGE.to_a - (db_used + docker_used).uniq\n    port = candidates.find { |p| port_free?(p) }\n    port || raise(Error, \"No SSH ports available in range #{Sandbox::SSH_PORT_RANGE}\")\n  end\n\n  def docker_bound_ssh_ports\n    Docker::Container.all(all: true).flat_map do |c|\n      (c.info[\"Ports\"] || []).map { |p| p[\"PublicPort\"] }.compact\n    end.select { |p| Sandbox::SSH_PORT_RANGE.include?(p) }\n  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError\n    []\n  end\n\n  # Verify a port is truly free by attempting a TCP connection to the Docker host.\n  # Returns true (free) if the connection is refused. Falls back to true if the\n  # Docker host is not reachable (e.g. production Linux without host.docker.internal).\n  def port_free?(port)\n    Timeout.timeout(0.3) { TCPSocket.new(\"host.docker.internal\", port).close }\n    false # connected → port is occupied\n  rescue Errno::ECONNREFUSED, Timeout::Error\n    true  # refused/timed-out → port is free\n  rescue SocketError, Errno::EHOSTUNREACH, Errno::ENETUNREACH\n    true  # host not reachable → skip TCP check, rely on Docker API result\n  end\n\n  def container_env(user, sandbox)",
  "new_string": "  def connect_to_network(container)\n    network = Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)\n    network.connect(container.id)\n  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError\n    Rails.logger.warn(\"SandboxManager: network #{NETWORK_NAME} not found, skipping network connection\")\n  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e\n    Rails.logger.warn(\"SandboxManager: failed to connect container to #{NETWORK_NAME}: #{e.message}\")\n  end\n\n  def container_env(user, sandbox)"
}

   525→  def connect_info(sandbox:)
   526→    user = sandbox.user.name
   527→
   528→    unless sandbox.tailscale?
   529→      raise Error, "SSH access requires Tailscale. Enable Tailscale on your account and connect this sandbox to your tailnet."
   530→    end
   531→
   532→    ts_ip = wait_for_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox)
   533→    raise Error, "Tailscale IP not available — is the Tailscale sidecar running?" unless ts_ip.present?
   534→
   535→    {
   536→      host: ts_ip,
   537→      port: 22,
   538→      user: user,
   539→      command: "ssh #{user}@#{ts_ip}",
   540→      tailscale_ip: ts_ip
   541→    }
   542→  end
   543→
   544→  private
   545→
   546→  def wait_for_tailscale_ip(sandbox:, max_attempts: 30, delay: 0.5)
   547→    # Wait for the sandbox to be provisioned (background job) and Tailscale IP to be assigned
   548→    max_attempts.times do
   549→      sandbox.reload # Refresh from DB to get latest status
   550→
   551→      # If sandbox isn't running yet, keep waiting (provision job in progress)
   552→      if sandbox.status != "running"
   553→        sleep delay
   554→        next
   555→      end
   556→
   557→      # Sandbox is running, try to get Tailscale IP
   558→      ts_ip = TailscaleManager.new.sandbox_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox)
   559→      return ts_ip if ts_ip.present?
   560→
   561→      sleep delay
   562→    end
   563→    Rails.logger.warn("Tailscale IP not available for sandbox #{sandbox.id} after #{max_attempts} attempts (status: #{sandbox.status})")
   564→    nil
   565→  end
   566→
   567→  def connect_to_network(container)
   568→    network = Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   569→    network.connect(container.id)
   570→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   571→    Rails.logger.warn("SandboxManager: network #{NETWORK_NAME} not found, skipping network connection")
   572→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
   573→    Rails.logger.warn("SandboxManager: failed to connect container to #{NETWORK_NAME}: #{e.message}")
   574→  end
   575→
   576→  def container_env(user, sandbox)
   577→    env = [
   578→      "SANDCASTLE_USER=#{user.name}",
   579→      "SANDCASTLE_SSH_KEY=#{user.ssh_public_key}"
   580→    ]
   581→    env << "USER_EMAIL=#{user.email_address}" if user.email_address.present?
   582→    env << "USER_FULLNAME=#{user.full_name}" if user.full_name.present?
   583→    env << "SANDCASTLE_VNC_ENABLED=#{sandbox.vnc_enabled? ? '1' : '0'}"
   584→    env << "SANDCASTLE_VNC_GEOMETRY=#{sandbox.vnc_geometry}"
   585→    env << "SANDCASTLE_VNC_DEPTH=#{sandbox.vnc_depth}"
   586→    env
   587→  end
   588→
   589→  def container_runtime
   590→    @container_runtime ||= begin
   591→      runtimes = Docker.info["Runtimes"] || {}
   592→      if runtimes.key?("sysbox-runc")
   593→        "sysbox-runc"
   594→      else
   595→        Rails.logger.warn("SandboxManager: sysbox-runc not available, falling back to runc (Docker-in-Docker will not work inside sandboxes)")
   596→        "runc"
   597→      end
   598→    end
   599→  end
   600→
   601→  def volume_binds(user, sandbox)
   602→    binds = []
   603→    if sandbox.mount_home
   604→      binds << "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/home:/home/#{user.name}"
   605→    end
   606→    if sandbox.persistent_volume && sandbox.volume_path
   607→      binds << "#{sandbox.volume_path}:/workspace"
   608→    end
   609→    if sandbox.data_path.present?

   179→              Data directory (<%= @sandbox.data_path %>)

   181→          <% end %>

     1→class Sandbox < ApplicationRecord
     2→  include ActionView::RecordIdentifier  # For dom_id in Turbo broadcasts
     3→
     4→  belongs_to :user
     5→  has_many :routes, dependent: :destroy
     6→
     7→  VNC_GEOMETRIES = %w[1280x900 1366x768 1440x900 1600x900 1920x1080 2560x1440].freeze
     8→  VNC_DEPTHS = [ 8, 16, 24, 32 ].freeze
     9→
    10→  validates :name, presence: true,
    11→    uniqueness: { scope: :user_id, conditions: -> { where.not(status: "destroyed") } },
    12→    format: { with: /\A[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,62}\z/, message: "must be lowercase alphanumeric" }
    13→  validates :status, inclusion: { in: %w[pending running stopped destroyed] }
    14→  validates :image, presence: true
    15→  validates :vnc_geometry, inclusion: { in: VNC_GEOMETRIES }
    16→  validates :vnc_depth, inclusion: { in: VNC_DEPTHS }
    17→
    18→  scope :active, -> { where.not(status: "destroyed") }
    19→  scope :running, -> { where(status: "running") }
    20→
    21→  # Turbo Streams for real-time UI updates
    22→  after_create_commit :broadcast_prepend_to_dashboard
    23→  after_update_commit :broadcast_replace_to_dashboard
    24→  after_destroy_commit :broadcast_remove_from_dashboard
    25→
    26→  def full_name
    27→    "#{user.name}-#{name}"
    28→  end
    29→
    30→  def connect_command
    31→    "sandcastle connect #{name}"
    32→  end
    33→
    34→  def routed?
    35→    routes.loaded? ? routes.any? : routes.exists?
    36→  end
    37→
    38→  def temp?
    39→    temporary?
    40→  end
    41→
    42→  # Job lifecycle management
    43→  def job_in_progress?
    44→    job_status.present?
    45→  end
    46→
    47→  def job_failed?
    48→    job_error.present?
    49→  end
    50→
    51→  def start_job(status)
    52→    update!(job_status: status, job_started_at: Time.current, job_error: nil)
    53→  end
    54→
    55→  def finish_job
    56→    update!(job_status: nil, job_started_at: nil)
    57→  end
    58→
    59→  def fail_job(error_message)
    60→    update!(job_status: nil, job_error: error_message, job_started_at: nil)
    61→  end
    62→
    63→  private
    64→
    65→  def broadcast_prepend_to_dashboard
    66→    broadcast_prepend_to(
    67→      [user, "dashboard"],
    68→      partial: "dashboard/sandbox",
    69→      locals: { sandbox: self },
    70→      target: "sandboxes"
    71→    )
    72→  end
    73→
    74→  def broadcast_replace_to_dashboard
    75→    # If sandbox is destroyed, remove it from the dashboard instead of replacing
    76→    if status == "destroyed"
    77→      broadcast_remove_to([user, "dashboard"], target: dom_id(self))
    78→    else
    79→      broadcast_replace_to(
    80→        [user, "dashboard"],
    81→        partial: "dashboard/sandbox",
    82→        locals: { sandbox: self },
    83→        target: dom_id(self)
    84→      )
    85→    end
    86→  end
    87→
    88→  def broadcast_remove_from_dashboard
    89→    broadcast_remove_to([user, "dashboard"], target: dom_id(self))
    90→  end
    91→
    92→end
    93→

> AGENT

Done. Here's a summary of everything that was changed:

**Rails (`app/services/sandbox_manager.rb`)**
- `create_container_and_start`: Removed `PortBindings` from Docker container config; calls new `connect_to_network()` after start to join the `sandcastle-web` bridge
- `start`: Removed port reassignment (`find_free_ssh_port` + `sandbox.update!(ssh_port:)`) — no port needed anymore
- `stop`: Removed `ssh_port: nil` from the status update
- `restore`: Same port removal + `connect_to_network()` after start
- `connect_info`: Rewritten to require Tailscale — raises `Error` if not enabled, waits for Tailscale IP and returns it on port 22
- Removed private methods: `find_free_ssh_port`, `docker_bound_ssh_ports`, `port_free?`
- Added `connect_to_network(container)` private method

**Rails (API / views)**
- `api/sandboxes_controller.rb`: Removed `ssh_port` from `sandbox_json`
- `api/users_controller.rb`: Removed `ssh_port` from inline sandbox list
- `sandboxes/show.html.erb`: Removed "· SSH port X" from the metadata line

**Go CLI**
- `api/types.go`: `SSHPort` now has `omitempty` (server no longer sends it)
- `cmd/sandbox.go`: `ls` output replaced `PORT` column with `CREATED` (formatted as `YYYY-MM-DD`)

Everything is complete. Here's the full picture of what was accomplished across this conversation:

**Done:**
1. Solid Errors added to admin navbar
2. `deploy:local` shows detailed error pages (`RAILS_SHOW_DETAILED_ERRORS`)
3. `web-console` added to local Docker deploy (`:local` gem group, `BUNDLE_WITHOUT=development:test`)
4. `db/errors_schema.rb` created so the errors DB works
5. TLS certs persisted via host bind mount (`.local/certs/`), survives `deploy:local:reset`
6. Sandbox containers labeled `sandcastle.sandbox=true` so `deploy:local:reset` can clean them up
7. **SSH port bindings fully removed:**
   - No more `PortBindings` in Docker container creation
   - Sandboxes join `sandcastle-web` network at start instead
   - CLI SSH requires Tailscale (`connect_info` raises if not enabled)
   - `sandcastle ls` now shows CREATED instead of PORT column
   - `ssh_port` removed from all API responses and views
   - Port-finding private methods removed from `SandboxManager`

> AGENT

The issue is that `network.disconnect` raises `Docker::Error::DockerError` (not `NotFoundError`) when the container isn't connected to the network. The inner rescue only catches `NotFoundError`, so the "not connected" error bubbles up to the outer rescue which re-raises it as a `TailscaleManager::Error`.

Fix: also rescue `DockerError` in the inner block for the disconnect call:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01QenZXdhtCPDM8nMnfeD7g6
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/tailscale_manager.rb",
  "old_string": "    if sandbox.container_id.present?\n      begin\n        network = Docker::Network.get(user.tailscale_network)\n        network.disconnect(sandbox.container_id)\n      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError\n        # Network or container already gone\n      end\n    end\n\n    sandbox.update!(tailscale: false)\n    sandbox\n  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e\n    raise Error, \"Failed to disconnect sandbox from Tailscale: #{e.message}\"\n  end",
  "new_string": "    if sandbox.container_id.present?\n      begin\n        network = Docker::Network.get(user.tailscale_network)\n        network.disconnect(sandbox.container_id)\n      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError, Docker::Error::DockerError\n        # Network/container already gone or container not connected — desired state reached\n      end\n    end\n\n    sandbox.update!(tailscale: false)\n    sandbox\n  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e\n    raise Error, \"Failed to disconnect sandbox from Tailscale: #{e.message}\"\n  end"
}
```

> AGENT

Pushed. The fix makes the inner rescue catch both `NotFoundError` and `DockerError` — so if a sandbox's container was never actually connected to the Tailscale bridge (e.g. existing sandboxes created before the `tailscale: true` flag was set, or ones where the connection failed silently), destroy no longer blows up.

     1→class VncManager
     2→  DATA_DIR = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR", "/data")
     3→  NETWORK_NAME = "sandcastle-web"
     4→  DYNAMIC_DIR = File.join(DATA_DIR, "traefik", "dynamic")
     5→
     6→  class Error < StandardError; end
     7→
     8→  # Opens a web-based VNC session for the given sandbox.
     9→  # Returns the URL path to the noVNC session.
    10→  # websockify runs inside the sandbox container (started by entrypoint.sh),
    11→  # so we only need to connect the sandbox to the Traefik network and write
    12→  # the Traefik routing config.
    13→  def open(sandbox:)
    14→    raise Error, "Sandbox is not running" unless sandbox.status == "running"
    15→    raise Error, "Sandbox has no container" if sandbox.container_id.blank?
    16→
    17→    ensure_network
    18→    connect_sandbox_to_network(sandbox)
    19→    write_traefik_config(sandbox)
    20→
    21→    vnc_url(sandbox)
    22→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    23→    raise Error, "Failed to open browser: #{e.message}"
    24→  rescue SystemCallError => e
    25→    raise Error, "Failed to open browser: #{e.message}"
    26→  end
    27→
    28→  # Closes the web VNC session for the given sandbox.
    29→  def close(sandbox:)
    30→    delete_traefik_config(sandbox)
    31→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    32→    Rails.logger.error("VncManager: close failed for #{sandbox.full_name}: #{e.message}")
    33→  end
    34→

    36→  # Requires the sandbox to already be connected to the sandcastle-web network
    37→  # (i.e., after open has been called).
    38→  def active?(sandbox:)
    39→    return false unless File.exist?(traefik_config_path(sandbox))
    40→
    41→    TCPSocket.new(sandbox.full_name, 6080).close
    42→    true
    43→  rescue Errno::ECONNREFUSED, Errno::ETIMEDOUT, SocketError
    44→    false
    45→  end
    46→
    47→  # Removes orphaned Traefik VNC configs whose sandbox is gone or not running.
    48→  def cleanup_orphaned
    49→    Dir.glob(File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "vnc-*.yml")).each do |path|
    50→      id = path.match(/vnc-(\d+)\.yml/)&.[](1)&.to_i
    51→      next unless id
    52→
    53→      sandbox = Sandbox.find_by(id: id)
    54→      if sandbox.nil? || sandbox.status != "running"
    55→        File.delete(path)
    56→        Rails.logger.info("VncManager: removed orphaned Traefik config #{File.basename(path)}")
    57→      end
    58→    end
    59→  rescue => e
    60→    Rails.logger.error("VncManager: orphan cleanup failed: #{e.message}")
    61→  end
    62→

    64→    write_traefik_config(sandbox)
    65→  end
    66→
    67→  private
    68→
    69→  def vnc_url(sandbox)
    70→    id = sandbox.id
    71→    "/vnc/#{id}/vnc.html?path=vnc/#{id}/websockify&autoconnect=true"
    72→  end
    73→
    74→  def traefik_config_path(sandbox)
    75→    File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "vnc-#{sandbox.id}.yml")
    76→  end
    77→
    78→  def ensure_network
    79→    Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
    80→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
    81→    Docker::Network.create(NETWORK_NAME, "Driver" => "bridge")
    82→  end
    83→
    84→  def connect_sandbox_to_network(sandbox)
    85→    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?
    86→
    87→    network = Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
    88→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
    89→
    90→    networks = container.json.dig("NetworkSettings", "Networks") || {}
    91→    return if networks.key?(NETWORK_NAME)
    92→
    93→    network.connect(sandbox.container_id)
    94→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
    95→    raise Error, "Sandbox container not found. Please refresh and try again."
    96→  end
    97→
    98→  def write_traefik_config(sandbox)
    99→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(DYNAMIC_DIR)
   100→    config_path = traefik_config_path(sandbox)
   101→    return if File.exist?(config_path)
   102→
   103→    host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")
   104→    id = sandbox.id
   105→    sandbox_name = sandbox.full_name
   106→
   107→    base_rule = if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   108→      "HostRegexp(`.+`) && PathPrefix(`/vnc/#{id}`)"
   109→    else
   110→      "Host(`#{host}`) && PathPrefix(`/vnc/#{id}`)"
   111→    end
   112→
   113→    # Route all /vnc/{id} traffic to the sandbox container's websockify process
   114→    # on port 6080. websockify serves noVNC static files and proxies WebSocket
   115→    # connections to Xvnc on localhost:5900 inside the sandbox.
   116→    # stripPrefix removes /vnc/{id} so websockify sees /websockify and /vnc.html.
   117→    config = {
   118→      "http" => {
   119→        "routers" => {
   120→          "vnc-#{id}" => {
   121→            "rule" => base_rule,
   122→            "service" => "vnc-#{id}",
   123→            "entryPoints" => [ "websecure" ],
   124→            "tls" => tls_config,
   125→            "middlewares" => [ "vnc-auth-#{id}", "vnc-stripprefix-#{id}" ],
   126→            "priority" => 100
   127→          }
   128→        },
   129→        "middlewares" => {

   131→            "forwardAuth" => {
   132→              "address" => "http://sandcastle-web:80/vnc/auth",
   133→              "trustForwardHeader" => true
   134→            }
   135→          },
   136→          "vnc-stripprefix-#{id}" => {
   137→            "stripPrefix" => {
   138→              "prefixes" => [ "/vnc/#{id}" ]
   139→            }
   140→          }
   141→        },
   142→        "services" => {
   143→          "vnc-#{id}" => {
   144→            "loadBalancer" => {
   145→              "servers" => [ { "url" => "http://#{sandbox_name}:6080" } ]
   146→            }
   147→          }
   148→        }
   149→      }
   150→    }
   151→
   152→    File.write(config_path, config.to_yaml)
   153→  end
   154→
   155→  def tls_config
   156→    if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   157→      {}
   158→    else
   159→      { "certResolver" => "letsencrypt" }
   160→    end
   161→  end
   162→
   163→  def delete_traefik_config(sandbox)

   165→    File.delete(path) if File.exist?(path)

# the RFB banner immediately on connect — required for websockify compatibility.
# x11vnc 0.9.17+ waits for client data before sending the banner, deadlocking
# with websockify which also waits for the server to speak first.
# Both processes run as $USERNAME (not root) for proper display ownership.
VNC_ENABLED="${SANDCASTLE_VNC_ENABLED:-1}"
VNC_GEOMETRY="${SANDCASTLE_VNC_GEOMETRY:-1280x900}"
VNC_DEPTH="${SANDCASTLE_VNC_DEPTH:-24}"

```
if command -v Xvnc &>/dev/null && [ "$VNC_ENABLED" = "1" ]; then
    touch /var/log/xvnc.log /var/log/openbox.log
    chown "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" /var/log/xvnc.log /var/log/openbox.log
    su -s /bin/bash "$USERNAME" -c \
        "Xvnc :99 -rfbport 5900 -SecurityTypes None -AlwaysShared -geometry ${VNC_GEOMETRY} -depth ${VNC_DEPTH} &>/var/log/xvnc.log &"
    # Start Openbox window manager once the display is ready
    if command -v openbox &>/dev/null; then
        su -s /bin/bash "$USERNAME" -c \
            'DISPLAY=:99 openbox &>/var/log/openbox.log &'
    fi
    # Export DISPLAY for all SSH sessions via PAM environment
    echo "DISPLAY=:99" >> /etc/environment
fi

# Start SSH daemon in foreground
exec /usr/sbin/sshd -D -e
```

commit 972d61d0868413bda8a2982053d76a3440a3a6e1
Author: thieso2 <thieso@gmail.com>
Date:   Sat Feb 21 12:05:48 2026 +0100

    feat(vnc): run websockify inside sandbox container instead of sidecar (#46)
    
    Install novnc and python3-websockify in the sandbox image (GUI layer) so
    each sandbox starts websockify on port 6080 at boot, proxying WebSocket
    connections to Xvnc on localhost:5900.
    
    Remove the sc-vnc-{name} sidecar container lifecycle from VncManager:
    no more image pull, container create/start/cleanup. open() now just
    connects the sandbox to sandcastle-web and writes the Traefik config;
    close() just deletes the config. cleanup_orphaned() scans for stale
    Traefik config files instead of stale containers. Traefik routes to
    {sandbox.full_name}:6080 instead of sc-vnc-{name}:6080.
    
    Benefits: ~1s session open vs 3-4s, no orphaned containers, ~60 fewer
    lines of code in VncManager, fewer moving parts.
    
    Co-authored-by: Claude Sonnet 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>

 app/services/vnc_manager.rb  | 153 ++++++++-----------------------------------
 images/sandbox/Dockerfile    |   6 +-
 images/sandbox/entrypoint.sh |   4 ++
 3 files changed, 36 insertions(+), 127 deletions(-)
```

The old container is removed. You can now recreate the sandbox from the UI or CLI — it will use the new image with websockify on port 6080 and VNC should work.

**Root cause:** The `feat(vnc): run websockify inside sandbox container` commit (merged today) changed the architecture so websockify runs inside the sandbox at boot instead of as a sidecar. But the locally cached sandbox image was from 2026-02-19, before that change. The registry doesn't have an arm64 build yet (needs a release tag), so I built the image locally from the updated Dockerfile.

  "command": "git commit -m \"fix(traefik): use empty TLS config for mkcert mode in VncManager and TerminalManager\n\nmkcert mode uses a pre-generated cert loaded via tls.yml, not letsencrypt.\nOnly omit certResolver when TLS_MODE is selfsigned or mkcert; anything\nelse (production) falls through to letsencrypt.\n\nStale vnc/terminal Traefik configs with certResolver=letsencrypt were\ncausing Traefik to reject all routes and serve 404 for the whole site.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>\" -- app/services/vnc_manager.rb app/services/terminal_manager.rb && git push",

> AGENT

All errors are from 13:02 (old). The dynamic dir is clean with only `rails.yml` + `tls.yml`. The site should be back up.

**Summary of what happened:**
1. An earlier VNC/terminal session wrote `vnc-3.yml` and `terminal-3.yml` to Traefik's dynamic config with `certResolver: letsencrypt` — wrong for local `mkcert` mode
2. When Traefik restarted, it rejected those routers (no letsencrypt in local setup), which broke routing for **everything** including the main Rails route
3. **Immediate fix:** deleted the stale config files
4. **Root cause fix:** `tls_config` in both `VncManager` and `TerminalManager` now treats `mkcert` the same as `selfsigned` (empty TLS config, uses the cert from `tls.yml`)

Found it — the sandbox is on the default `bridge` network (all containers auto-join it), so two sandboxes can reach each other through it. The fix is to specify `sandcastle-web` as the network at container creation time, which prevents Docker from adding it to the default bridge.

> AGENT

Done. Two fixes in one commit:

**Network isolation:** Sandbox containers now specify `NetworkMode: sandcastle-web` and `NetworkingConfig` at creation time, so Docker never adds them to the default `bridge`. Previously every sandbox auto-joined the shared `bridge` network (the same one used by unrelated containers on the host), meaning two sandboxes could talk to each other through it. Now each sandbox is only on `sandcastle-web` (plus Tailscale if enabled).

**CREATED column:** Now shows `2026-02-21 13:04` (local time) instead of just the date.

> AGENT

The subnet is derived deterministically from user ID: user ID 1 → `10.89.2.0/24` on both installs, so both sandboxes land on `10.89.2.3`. The fix: if the Tailscale bridge network already exists, read its actual subnet; if not, pick a random `/24` from the pool. No DB migration needed:

> TOOL

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/tailscale_manager.rb",
  "old_string": "  def subnet_for(user)\n    base = ENV.fetch(\"DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE\", \"10.89.0.0/16\")\n    # Derive per-user /24 from the /16 pool allocated by dockyard\n    # e.g. base 10.89.0.0/16, user_id 1 → 10.89.1.0/24, user_id 2 → 10.89.2.0/24\n    # This ensures Tailscale networks are within DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE so dockyard's NAT rules apply\n    parts = base.split(\"/\").first.split(\".\").map(&:to_i)\n    offset = 1 + (user.id % 255)  # 1-255 to avoid .0 subnet collision\n    parts[2] = offset\n    \"#{parts[0]}.#{parts[1]}.#{parts[2]}.0/24\"\n  end",
  "new_string": "  def subnet_for(user)\n    # If the network already exists, use its actual subnet (stable across restarts)\n    network = Docker::Network.get(\"sc-ts-net-#{user.name}\")\n    ipam = network.info.dig(\"IPAM\", \"Config\")\n    return ipam.first[\"Subnet\"] if ipam&.first\n  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError\n    # Network doesn't exist yet — pick a random /24 within the pool\n  end\n\n  def subnet_for(user)\n    base = ENV.fetch(\"DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE\", \"10.89.0.0/16\")\n    parts = base.split(\"/\").first.split(\".\").map(&:to_i)\n    parts[2] = rand(1..254)\n    \"#{parts[0]}.#{parts[1]}.#{parts[2]}.0/24\"\n  end"
}

  "new_string": "  def subnet_for(user)\n    # If the network already exists, read its actual subnet (stable across restarts)\n    begin\n      network = Docker::Network.get(\"sc-ts-net-#{user.name}\")\n      ipam = network.info.dig(\"IPAM\", \"Config\")\n      return ipam.first[\"Subnet\"] if ipam&.first\n    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError\n      # Network doesn't exist yet — fall through to generate a random /24\n    end\n\n    base = ENV.fetch(\"DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE\", \"10.89.0.0/16\")\n    parts = base.split(\"/\").first.split(\".\").map(&:to_i)\n    parts[2] = rand(1..254)\n    \"#{parts[0]}.#{parts[1]}.#{parts[2]}.0/24\"\n  end"

If not, picks a random /24 from within DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE\n\nPreviously user_id 1 always → 10.89.2.0/24 on every install, so two\nSandcastle instances would advertise the same subnet to the same tailnet\nand both sandboxes would get IP 10.89.2.3 — causing routing conflicts\nand confusing CLI output.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>\" && git push",

> AGENT

Pushed. The fix:

- **On first Tailscale enable** (network doesn't exist yet): picks a random `/24` from the pool (e.g. `10.89.147.0/24`), so two separate installs will almost certainly get different subnets and different sandbox IPs
- **On subsequent runs** (network already exists): reads the subnet directly from Docker's IPAM config, so it's stable across restarts without needing a DB column

To take effect on your existing installs you'll need to re-enable Tailscale (or delete and recreate the `sc-ts-net-thies` network) so a new random subnet is assigned.

> DEVELOPER

does this need changes in dockyard? or is this tailscale only?

> AGENT

No Dockyard changes needed. The random subnet still stays within `DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE` (`10.89.0.0/16`) — we only randomize the third octet while keeping the first two fixed:

```
10.89.[random].0/24  ← still inside 10.89.0.0/16
```

Dockyard's NAT rules apply to the entire `/16` pool, so any `/24` carved out of it is automatically covered. The old code had an explicit comment about this requirement, which is why it used the deterministic approach — but the random approach satisfies the same constraint.

> AGENT

When `start` recreates the container, it only connects it to `sandcastle-web` — it doesn't reconnect to the Tailscale bridge. The `restore` method already does this correctly. The fix is the same one-liner:

    15→
    16→func verbose() bool {
    17→	// Check both VERBOSE and SANDCASTLE_VERBOSE
    18→	v := strings.ToLower(os.Getenv("VERBOSE"))
    19→	if v == "" {
    20→		v = strings.ToLower(os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_VERBOSE"))
    21→	}
    22→	return v == "1" || v == "true"
    23→}
    24→
    25→func logVerbose(format string, args ...any) {
    26→	if verbose() {
    27→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\033[2m"+format+"\033[0m\n", args...)
    28→	}
    29→}
    30→
    31→type Client struct {
    32→	BaseURL     string
    33→	Token       string
    34→	ServerAlias string
    35→	HTTPClient  *http.Client
    36→}
    37→
    38→func newHTTPClient(insecure bool) *http.Client {
    39→	if insecure {
    40→		return &http.Client{
    41→			Transport: &http.Transport{
    42→				TLSClientConfig: &tls.Config{InsecureSkipVerify: true},
    43→			},
    44→		}
    45→	}
    46→	return &http.Client{}
    47→}
    48→
    49→func NewClient() (*Client, error) {
    50→	cfg, err := config.Load()
    51→	if err != nil {
    52→		return nil, err
    53→	}
    54→	srv, err := cfg.CurrentServerConfig()
    55→	if err != nil {
    56→		return nil, err
    57→	}
    58→	logVerbose("server: %s (%s)", cfg.CurrentServer, srv.URL)
    59→	return &Client{
    60→		BaseURL:     srv.URL,
    61→		Token:       srv.Token,
    62→		ServerAlias: cfg.CurrentServer,
    63→		HTTPClient:  newHTTPClient(srv.Insecure),
    64→	}, nil
    65→}
    66→
    67→func NewClientWithToken(baseURL, token string, insecure bool) *Client {
    68→	return &Client{
    69→		BaseURL:    baseURL,
    70→		Token:      token,
    71→		HTTPClient: newHTTPClient(insecure),
    72→	}
    73→}
    74→
    75→func (c *Client) do(method, path string, body any, result any) error {
    76→	var bodyReader io.Reader
    77→	var reqData []byte
    78→	if body != nil {
    79→		var err error
    80→		reqData, err = json.Marshal(body)
    81→		if err != nil {
    82→			return fmt.Errorf("marshaling request: %w", err)
    83→		}
    84→		bodyReader = bytes.NewReader(reqData)
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	logVerbose("→ %s %s%s", method, c.BaseURL, path)
    88→	if len(reqData) > 0 {
    89→		logVerbose("  request: %s", string(reqData))
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	req, err := http.NewRequest(method, c.BaseURL+path, bodyReader)
    93→	if err != nil {
    94→		return fmt.Errorf("creating request: %w", err)
    95→	}
    96→
    97→	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
    98→	if c.Token != "" {
    99→		req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+c.Token)
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	resp, err := c.HTTPClient.Do(req)
   103→	if err != nil {
   104→		return fmt.Errorf("request failed: %w", err)
   105→	}
   106→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   107→
   108→	respBody, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
   109→	if err != nil {
   110→		return fmt.Errorf("reading response: %w", err)
   111→	}
   112→
   113→	logVerbose("← %d (%d bytes)", resp.StatusCode, len(respBody))
   114→	if len(respBody) > 0 {
   115→		logVerbose("  response: %s", string(respBody))
   116→	}
   117→
   118→	if resp.StatusCode >= 400 {
   119→		var apiErr APIError
   120→		if json.Unmarshal(respBody, &apiErr) == nil && apiErr.Error != "" {
   121→			return fmt.Errorf("API error (%d): %s", resp.StatusCode, apiErr.Error)
   122→		}
   123→		return fmt.Errorf("API error (%d): %s", resp.StatusCode, string(respBody))
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	if result != nil {
   127→		if err := json.Unmarshal(respBody, result); err != nil {
   128→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing response: %w", err)
   129→		}
   130→	}
   131→	return nil
   132→}
   133→
   134→// doWithStatus is like do but returns the HTTP status code along with the response body.
   135→func (c *Client) doWithStatus(method, path string, body any) (int, []byte, error) {
   136→	var bodyReader io.Reader
   137→	var reqData []byte
   138→	if body != nil {
   139→		var err error
   140→		reqData, err = json.Marshal(body)
   141→		if err != nil {
   142→			return 0, nil, fmt.Errorf("marshaling request: %w", err)
   143→		}
   144→		bodyReader = bytes.NewReader(reqData)
   145→	}
   146→
   147→	logVerbose("→ %s %s%s", method, c.BaseURL, path)
   148→	if len(reqData) > 0 {
   149→		logVerbose("  request: %s", string(reqData))
   150→	}
   151→
   152→	req, err := http.NewRequest(method, c.BaseURL+path, bodyReader)
   153→	if err != nil {
   154→		return 0, nil, fmt.Errorf("creating request: %w", err)
   155→	}
   156→
   157→	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   158→	if c.Token != "" {
   159→		req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+c.Token)
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	resp, err := c.HTTPClient.Do(req)
   163→	if err != nil {
   164→		return 0, nil, fmt.Errorf("request failed: %w", err)
   165→	}
   166→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   167→
   168→	respBody, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		return 0, nil, fmt.Errorf("reading response: %w", err)
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	logVerbose("← %d (%d bytes)", resp.StatusCode, len(respBody))
   174→	if len(respBody) > 0 {
   175→		logVerbose("  response: %s", string(respBody))
   176→	}
   177→
   178→	return resp.StatusCode, respBody, nil
   179→}

   189→func (c *Client) PollDeviceToken(deviceCode string) (token string, pending bool, err error) {
   190→	status, body, err := c.doWithStatus("POST", "/api/auth/device_token", DeviceTokenRequest{DeviceCode: deviceCode})
   191→	if err != nil {
   192→		return "", false, err
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	switch status {
   196→	case 200:
   197→		var resp DeviceTokenResponse
   198→		if err := json.Unmarshal(body, &resp); err != nil {
   199→			return "", false, fmt.Errorf("parsing response: %w", err)
   200→		}
   201→		return resp.Token, false, nil
   202→	case 428: // Precondition Required — authorization_pending
   203→		return "", true, nil
   204→	default:
   205→		var apiErr APIError
   206→		if json.Unmarshal(body, &apiErr) == nil && apiErr.Error != "" {
   207→			return "", false, fmt.Errorf("%s", apiErr.Error)
   208→		}
   209→		return "", false, fmt.Errorf("unexpected status %d: %s", status, string(body))
   210→	}
   211→}

     1→package config
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"os"
     6→	"path/filepath"
     7→
     8→	"gopkg.in/yaml.v3"
     9→)
    10→
    11→type ServerConfig struct {
    12→	URL      string `yaml:"url"`
    13→	Token    string `yaml:"token"`
    14→	Insecure bool   `yaml:"insecure,omitempty"`
    15→}
    16→
    17→type Config struct {
    18→	CurrentServer string                  `yaml:"current_server"`
    19→	Servers       map[string]ServerConfig `yaml:"servers"`
    20→}
    21→
    22→// legacyConfig is the old flat format for migration.
    23→type legacyConfig struct {
    24→	Server string `yaml:"server"`
    25→	Token  string `yaml:"token"`
    26→}
    27→
    28→func Dir() string {
    29→	home, _ := os.UserHomeDir()
    30→	return filepath.Join(home, ".sandcastle")
    31→}
    32→
    33→func Path() string {
    34→	return filepath.Join(Dir(), "config.yaml")
    35→}
    36→
    37→func Load() (*Config, error) {
    38→	cfg := &Config{Servers: make(map[string]ServerConfig)}
    39→	data, err := os.ReadFile(Path())
    40→	if err != nil {
    41→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
    42→			return cfg, nil
    43→		}
    44→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading config: %w", err)
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	// Try new format first
    48→	if err := yaml.Unmarshal(data, cfg); err != nil {
    49→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing config: %w", err)
    50→	}
    51→
    52→	// Migrate legacy flat format
    53→	if cfg.Servers == nil || len(cfg.Servers) == 0 {
    54→		var legacy legacyConfig
    55→		if err := yaml.Unmarshal(data, &legacy); err == nil && legacy.Server != "" {
    56→			cfg.Servers = map[string]ServerConfig{
    57→				"default": {URL: legacy.Server, Token: legacy.Token},
    58→			}
    59→			cfg.CurrentServer = "default"
    60→			// Save migrated config
    61→			_ = Save(cfg)
    62→		}
    63→	}
    64→
    65→	if cfg.Servers == nil {
    66→		cfg.Servers = make(map[string]ServerConfig)
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	return cfg, nil
    70→}
    71→
    72→func Save(cfg *Config) error {
    73→	if err := os.MkdirAll(Dir(), 0o700); err != nil {
    74→		return fmt.Errorf("creating config dir: %w", err)
    75→	}
    76→	data, err := yaml.Marshal(cfg)
    77→	if err != nil {
    78→		return fmt.Errorf("marshaling config: %w", err)
    79→	}
    80→	if err := os.WriteFile(Path(), data, 0o600); err != nil {
    81→		return fmt.Errorf("writing config: %w", err)
    82→	}
    83→	return nil
    84→}
    85→
    86→// CurrentServerConfig returns the active server's URL and token.
    87→func (c *Config) CurrentServerConfig() (ServerConfig, error) {
    88→	if c.CurrentServer == "" {
    89→		return ServerConfig{}, fmt.Errorf("no server configured — run: sandcastle login <url>")
    90→	}
    91→	srv, ok := c.Servers[c.CurrentServer]
    92→	if !ok {
    93→		return ServerConfig{}, fmt.Errorf("server %q not found in config — run: sandcastle login <url>", c.CurrentServer)
    94→	}
    95→	return srv, nil
    96→}
    97→
    98→// SetServer adds or updates a server and sets it as current.
    99→func (c *Config) SetServer(alias, url, token string, insecure bool) {
   100→	if c.Servers == nil {
   101→		c.Servers = make(map[string]ServerConfig)
   102→	}
   103→	c.Servers[alias] = ServerConfig{URL: url, Token: token, Insecure: insecure}
   104→	c.CurrentServer = alias
   105→}
   106→
   107→

  "new_string": "func NewClient() (*Client, error) {\n\tcfg, err := config.Load()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\t// SANDCASTLE_HOST overrides the active server — accepts a server alias or URL.\n\tif host := os.Getenv(\"SANDCASTLE_HOST\"); host != \"\" {\n\t\t// 1. Alias match\n\t\tif srv, ok := cfg.Servers[host]; ok {\n\t\t\tlogVerbose(\"server (SANDCASTLE_HOST alias): %s (%s)\", host, srv.URL)\n\t\t\treturn &Client{\n\t\t\t\tBaseURL:     srv.URL,\n\t\t\t\tToken:       srv.Token,\n\t\t\t\tServerAlias: host,\n\t\t\t\tHTTPClient:  newHTTPClient(srv.Insecure),\n\t\t\t}, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\t// 2. URL match — reuse stored token for that server\n\t\tnormalized := strings.TrimRight(host, \"/\")\n\t\tfor alias, srv := range cfg.Servers {\n\t\t\tif strings.TrimRight(srv.URL, \"/\") == normalized {\n\t\t\t\tlogVerbose(\"server (SANDCASTLE_HOST url): %s (%s)\", alias, normalized)\n\t\t\t\treturn &Client{\n\t\t\t\t\tBaseURL:     normalized,\n\t\t\t\t\tToken:       srv.Token,\n\t\t\t\t\tServerAlias: alias,\n\t\t\t\t\tHTTPClient:  newHTTPClient(srv.Insecure),\n\t\t\t\t}, nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\t// 3. Unknown URL — use without auth\n\t\tlogVerbose(\"server (SANDCASTLE_HOST, unauthenticated): %s\", normalized)\n\t\treturn &Client{\n\t\t\tBaseURL:    normalized,\n\t\t\tHTTPClient: newHTTPClient(false),\n\t\t}, nil\n\t}\n\n\tsrv, err := cfg.CurrentServerConfig()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tlogVerbose(\"server: %s (%s)\", cfg.CurrentServer, srv.URL)\n\treturn &Client{\n\t\tBaseURL:     srv.URL,\n\t\tToken:       srv.Token,\n\t\tServerAlias: cfg.CurrentServer,\n\t\tHTTPClient:  newHTTPClient(srv.Insecure),\n\t}, nil\n}"
}

   194→    </div>

   196→      <%= f.text_field :name,
   197→            required: true,
   198→            id: "snap-name-#{sandbox.id}",
   199→            pattern: "[a-z][a-z0-9_\\-]*",
   200→            class: "w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500 text-sm font-mono" %>
   201→      <div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-3">

   203→                class="px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-900">Cancel</button>
   204→        <%= f.submit "Create Snapshot", class: "px-4 py-1.5 bg-blue-600 text-white text-sm font-medium rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 cursor-pointer" %>
   205→      </div>
   206→    <% end %>
   207→  </div>
   208→</div>

```
     1→class DashboardController < ApplicationController
     2→  def index
     3→    @sandboxes = policy_scope(Sandbox).includes(:user, :routes).order(:name)
     4→    @vnc_active_names = running_vnc_names
     5→  end
     6→
     7→  def card
     8→    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).includes(:user, :routes).find(params[:id])
     9→    authorize sandbox
    10→    vnc_active = VncManager.new.active?(sandbox: sandbox)
    11→    render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(helpers.dom_id(sandbox), partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: sandbox, vnc_active: vnc_active })
    12→  end
    13→
    14→  def stats
    15→    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])
    16→    authorize sandbox, :stats?
    17→
    18→    if sandbox.status == "running" && sandbox.container_id.present?
    19→      container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
    20→      raw = container.stats(stream: false)
    21→
    22→      # Handle case where container is shutting down and returns nil/incomplete stats
    23→      if raw.present?
    24→        networks = raw["networks"] || {}
    25→        net_rx = networks.values.sum { |n| n["rx_bytes"] || 0 }
    26→        net_tx = networks.values.sum { |n| n["tx_bytes"] || 0 }
    27→
    28→        blkio = raw.dig("blkio_stats", "io_service_bytes_recursive") || []
    29→        disk_read = blkio.select { |e| e["op"]&.downcase == "read" }.sum { |e| e["value"] || 0 }
    30→        disk_write = blkio.select { |e| e["op"]&.downcase == "write" }.sum { |e| e["value"] || 0 }
    31→
    32→        @stats = {
    33→          cpu_percent: calculate_cpu_percent(raw),
    34→          memory_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "usage") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
    35→          memory_limit_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "limit") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
    36→          net_rx: net_rx,
    37→          net_tx: net_tx,
    38→          disk_read: disk_read,
    39→          disk_write: disk_write,
    40→          pids: raw.dig("pids_stats", "current") || 0
    41→        }
    42→      end
    43→    end
    44→
    45→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: @stats, sandbox: sandbox }
    46→  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound
    47→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: nil, sandbox: nil }
    48→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
    49→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: nil, sandbox: sandbox }
    50→  end
    51→
    52→  private
    53→
    54→  def running_vnc_names
    55→    Docker::Container.all.filter_map { |c|
    56→      name = c.info.dig("Names")&.first&.delete_prefix("/")
    57→      name.delete_prefix("sc-vnc-") if name&.start_with?("sc-vnc-")
    58→    }.to_set
    59→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
    60→    Set.new
    61→  end
    62→
    63→  def calculate_cpu_percent(stats)
    64→    cpu_delta = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "cpu_usage", "total_usage").to_f -
    65→                stats.dig("precpu_stats", "cpu_usage", "total_usage").to_f
    66→    system_delta = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "system_cpu_usage").to_f -
    67→                   stats.dig("precpu_stats", "system_cpu_usage").to_f
    68→    num_cpus = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "online_cpus") || 1
    69→
    70→    return 0.0 if system_delta.zero?
    71→    ((cpu_delta / system_delta) * num_cpus * 100.0).round(1)
    72→  end
    73→end
    74→

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/dashboard_controller.rb",
  "old_string": "  def running_vnc_names\n    Docker::Container.all.filter_map { |c|\n      name = c.info.dig(\"Names\")&.first&.delete_prefix(\"/\")\n      name.delete_prefix(\"sc-vnc-\") if name&.start_with?(\"sc-vnc-\")\n    }.to_set\n  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError\n    Set.new\n  end",
  "new_string": "  def running_vnc_ids\n    Dir.glob(File.join(VncManager::DYNAMIC_DIR, \"vnc-*.yml\")).filter_map { |f|\n      File.basename(f).match(/\\Avnc-(\\d+)\\.yml\\z/)&.[](1)&.to_i\n    }.to_set\n  rescue\n    Set.new\n  end\n\n  def tailscale_ips_for(sandboxes)\n    ts_sandboxes = sandboxes.select { |s| s.tailscale? && s.container_id.present? }\n    return {} if ts_sandboxes.empty?\n\n    all_containers = Docker::Container.all\n    container_map = all_containers.each_with_object({}) { |c, h| h[c.id] = c }\n\n    ts_sandboxes.each_with_object({}) do |sandbox, ips|\n      container = container_map[sandbox.container_id]\n      next unless container\n      network = sandbox.user.tailscale_network\n      next unless network.present?\n      ip = container.info.dig(\"NetworkSettings\", \"Networks\", network, \"IPAddress\")\n      ips[sandbox.id] = ip if ip.present?\n    end\n  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError\n    {}\n  end"
}

     1→class DashboardController < ApplicationController
     2→  def index
     3→    @sandboxes = policy_scope(Sandbox).includes(:user, :routes).order(:name)
     4→    @vnc_active_ids = running_vnc_ids
     5→    @tailscale_ips = tailscale_ips_for(@sandboxes)
     6→  end
     7→
     8→  def card
     9→    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).includes(:user, :routes).find(params[:id])
    10→    authorize sandbox
    11→    vnc_active = VncManager.new.active?(sandbox: sandbox)
    12→    tailscale_ip = sandbox.tailscale? ? TailscaleManager.new.sandbox_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox) : nil
    13→    render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(helpers.dom_id(sandbox), partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: sandbox, vnc_active: vnc_active, tailscale_ip: tailscale_ip })
    14→  end
    15→
    16→  def stats
    17→    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])
    18→    authorize sandbox, :stats?
    19→
    20→    if sandbox.status == "running" && sandbox.container_id.present?
    21→      container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
    22→      raw = container.stats(stream: false)
    23→
    24→      # Handle case where container is shutting down and returns nil/incomplete stats
    25→      if raw.present?
    26→        networks = raw["networks"] || {}
    27→        net_rx = networks.values.sum { |n| n["rx_bytes"] || 0 }
    28→        net_tx = networks.values.sum { |n| n["tx_bytes"] || 0 }
    29→
    30→        blkio = raw.dig("blkio_stats", "io_service_bytes_recursive") || []
    31→        disk_read = blkio.select { |e| e["op"]&.downcase == "read" }.sum { |e| e["value"] || 0 }
    32→        disk_write = blkio.select { |e| e["op"]&.downcase == "write" }.sum { |e| e["value"] || 0 }
    33→
    34→        @stats = {
    35→          cpu_percent: calculate_cpu_percent(raw),
    36→          memory_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "usage") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
    37→          memory_limit_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "limit") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
    38→          net_rx: net_rx,
    39→          net_tx: net_tx,
    40→          disk_read: disk_read,
    41→          disk_write: disk_write,
    42→          pids: raw.dig("pids_stats", "current") || 0
    43→        }
    44→      end
    45→    end
    46→
    47→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: @stats, sandbox: sandbox }
    48→  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound
    49→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: nil, sandbox: nil }
    50→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
    51→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: nil, sandbox: sandbox }
    52→  end
    53→
    54→  private
    55→
    56→  def running_vnc_ids
    57→    Dir.glob(File.join(VncManager::DYNAMIC_DIR, "vnc-*.yml")).filter_map { |f|
    58→      File.basename(f).match(/\Avnc-(\d+)\.yml\z/)&.[](1)&.to_i
    59→    }.to_set
    60→  rescue
    61→    Set.new
    62→  end
    63→
    64→  def tailscale_ips_for(sandboxes)
    65→    ts_sandboxes = sandboxes.select { |s| s.tailscale? && s.container_id.present? }
    66→    return {} if ts_sandboxes.empty?
    67→
    68→    all_containers = Docker::Container.all
    69→    container_map = all_containers.each_with_object({}) { |c, h| h[c.id] = c }
    70→
    71→    ts_sandboxes.each_with_object({}) do |sandbox, ips|
    72→      container = container_map[sandbox.container_id]
    73→      next unless container
    74→      network = sandbox.user.tailscale_network
    75→      next unless network.present?
    76→      ip = container.info.dig("NetworkSettings", "Networks", network, "IPAddress")
    77→      ips[sandbox.id] = ip if ip.present?
    78→    end
    79→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
    80→    {}
    81→  end
    82→
    83→  def calculate_cpu_percent(stats)
    84→    cpu_delta = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "cpu_usage", "total_usage").to_f -
    85→                stats.dig("precpu_stats", "cpu_usage", "total_usage").to_f
    86→    system_delta = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "system_cpu_usage").to_f -
    87→                   stats.dig("precpu_stats", "system_cpu_usage").to_f
    88→    num_cpus = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "online_cpus") || 1
    89→
    90→    return 0.0 if system_delta.zero?
    91→    ((cpu_delta / system_delta) * num_cpus * 100.0).round(1)
    92→  end
    93→end
    94→

+	// SANDCASTLE_HOST overrides the active server — accepts a server alias or URL.
+	if host := os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_HOST"); host != "" {
+		// 1. Alias match
+		if srv, ok := cfg.Servers[host]; ok {
+			logVerbose("server (SANDCASTLE_HOST alias): %s (%s)", host, srv.URL)
+			return &Client{
+				BaseURL:     srv.URL,
+				Token:       srv.Token,
+				ServerAlias: host,
+				HTTPClient:  newHTTPClient(srv.Insecure),
+			}, nil
+		}
+		// 2. URL match — reuse stored token for that server
+		normalized := strings.TrimRight(host, "/")
+		for alias, srv := range cfg.Servers {

+				logVerbose("server (SANDCASTLE_HOST url): %s (%s)", alias, normalized)
+				return &Client{
+					BaseURL:     normalized,
+					Token:       srv.Token,
+					ServerAlias: alias,
+					HTTPClient:  newHTTPClient(srv.Insecure),
+				}, nil
+			}
+		}
+		// 3. Unknown URL — use without auth
+		logVerbose("server (SANDCASTLE_HOST, unauthenticated): %s", normalized)
+		return &Client{
+			BaseURL:    normalized,
+			HTTPClient: newHTTPClient(false),
+		}, nil
+	}

> AGENT

Done. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

### `SANDCASTLE_HOST` env var (CLI)

In `NewClient()`, before falling through to the configured active server:
1. **Alias match** — if `SANDCASTLE_HOST=prod` matches a configured server alias, use that server's URL + token
2. **URL match** — if `SANDCASTLE_HOST=https://my.server.com` matches a configured server's URL, reuse that server's token
3. **Unknown URL** — if the URL isn't in config, connect without auth

### Dashboard improvements

**Tailscale IP on sandbox cards:**
- The Tailscale pill now shows the sandbox's IP on the Tailscale bridge: `Tailscale · 10.89.42.5`
- Fetched efficiently in one `Docker::Container.all` call for all Tailscale-enabled sandboxes
- The "browser" pill shown via `vnc_active` now correctly reflects whether a VNC Traefik config exists (i.e., the user has opened VNC for that sandbox), instead of the broken old check for `sc-vnc-*` sidecar containers that no longer exist

    59→  # Build the full VNC URL. In production, Traefik is the entry point
    60→  # so a relative path works. In local dev (selfsigned TLS), Rails may be
    61→  # accessed directly on a different port, so we need an absolute URL.
    62→  def vnc_redirect_url(path)

    69→  # Called by Traefik forwardAuth. Returns 200 to allow, or redirects to
    70→  # login (which Traefik passes through to the browser).
    71→  def auth
    72→    forwarded_uri = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Uri"] || ""
    73→    match = forwarded_uri.match(%r{/vnc/(\d+)})
    74→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless match
    75→
    76→    session_record = find_session_by_cookie
    77→    unless session_record
    78→      # Build the original VNC URL from Traefik's forwarded headers
    79→      # so the user returns here after logging in.
    80→      proto = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Proto"] || "https"
    81→      host  = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Host"] || request.host_with_port
    82→      original_url = "#{proto}://#{host}#{forwarded_uri}"
    83→      session[:return_to_after_authenticating] = original_url
    84→      redirect_to new_session_url, allow_other_host: true
    85→      return
    86→    end
    87→
    88→    user = session_record.user
    89→    sandbox = Sandbox.active.find_by(id: match[1].to_i)
    90→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless sandbox && (sandbox.user_id == user.id || user.admin?)
    91→
    92→    head :ok
    93→  end
    94→end

> AGENT

Here's what happened:

**First attempt (RoutingError):**
- The wait page was loaded via `http://dev.sand` (Rails directly on port 80)
- The VNC URL is built with `vnc_redirect_url()` — when no `SANDCASTLE_VNC_URL` env is set, it returns a relative path: `/vnc/6/vnc.html?...`
- The JS did `window.location.href = "/vnc/6/vnc.html?..."` → hit `http://dev.sand/vnc/6/vnc.html` → Rails received it → RoutingError (Rails has no route for that path — Traefik is supposed to handle `/vnc/*`)

**Reload worked because:**
- After the error, the browser was on the `https://dev.sand:8443` origin (Traefik's HTTPS endpoint), so the next navigation used the correct origin
- Traefik received `/vnc/6/vnc.html` → matched the `vnc-6` router rule (PathPrefix `/vnc/6`, priority 100) → called `/vnc/auth` on Rails (forwardAuth) → got 200 → stripped the prefix → forwarded to `sandbox-container:6080/vnc.html` → websockify served the noVNC page

**The fix:** Set `SANDCASTLE_VNC_URL=https://dev.sand:8443` in your dev env config so the redirect always uses the absolute Traefik URL, the same way `SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL` works for the terminal. Without it, the redirect is relative and only works if the wait page was already loaded through Traefik.

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me provide you with a concise summary:

## Summary

### **websockify-go Library**

**Purpose & How it Works:**
A pure Go implementation of the Python `novnc/websockify` TCP-to-WebSocket proxy. It proxies VNC/RFB protocol connections over WebSocket, enabling browser-based VNC clients (like noVNC) to connect to VNC servers. Simple, clean, written as educational/tutorial code demonstrating network proxy patterns in Go.

**API & Usage:**
- **Binary CLI:** Lightweight (~100 lines), easy to run standalone:
  ```bash
  websockify -addr :8080 -target localhost:5900 -url /vnc
  ```
- **Go Package:** Can be embedded via `go get github.com/ruzhila/websockify-go`
- **Features:** SSL/TLS support, custom URL paths, custom target addresses
- **Docker support:** Includes Dockerfile for containerization

**Dependencies:**
- Only **one external dependency:** `github.com/gorilla/websocket v1.5.1` (production)
- Test dependencies: testify, go-spew, go-difflib, yaml.v3

**License:** BSD 3-Clause

**Maturity & Activity:**
- ⭐ **6 stars** (low adoption)
- Created May 2024, last updated Jan 2026 (recent, but sporadic activity)
- 0 forks, 0 open issues
- **Small codebase:** 5 KB repository, minimal scope (single Go file + CLI)
- Educational/tutorial project — not heavily maintained production library

---

### **Current Sandcastle Setup**

**Dockerfile (lines 18–21):**
```dockerfile
RUN apt-get install -y \
    tigervnc-standalone-server openbox xterm xfonts-base xfonts-100dpi xfonts-75dpi \
    novnc python3-websockify
```

**entrypoint.sh (lines 84–104):**
- Launches **Xvnc** (TigerVNC) on port 5900 (RFB server)
- Launches **Openbox** window manager on display :99
- Launches **websockify** (Python package from apt) proxying WebSocket on port 6080 → localhost:5900:
  ```bash
  websockify --web /usr/share/novnc/ 6080 localhost:5900
  ```
- Exports `DISPLAY=:99` for SSH sessions

**Key Detail:** Currently using the **Python `websockify` package** (`python3-websockify`) from Ubuntu apt. Exposes ports 5900 (RFB) and 6080 (WebSocket/noVNC).

---

### **Why You Might Consider websockify-go**

✅ **Minimal dependencies** (only Gorilla WebSocket) vs. Python runtime bloat  
✅ **Faster startup & lower memory** (compiled binary vs. Python interpreter)  
✅ **Single static binary** (easier deployment in containerized environments)  
❌ **Very early stage** (low stars, educational project, minimal community)  
❌ **Risk:** Small project = potential for bugs, less battle-tested  

**Recommendation:** If you want to reduce image size/startup time, test websockify-go as a proof-of-concept. Pin a specific commit if you adopt it.

Issue created: https://github.com/thieso2/Sandcastle/issues/47

**Key points captured in the issue:**
- Current setup uses `python3-websockify` (Python runtime + apt packages, ~50 MB overhead)
- `websockify-go` is a single static binary with only one dependency (`gorilla/websocket`), far lower footprint
- Main risk: only 6 stars, educational-quality project — needs auditing and commit pinning
- The main open question is how to serve noVNC static files, since Python websockify handles that with `--web /usr/share/novnc/` but websockify-go doesn't have that flag — would need nginx or an embedded file server

> AGENT

Yes, absolutely — and it's actually the cleaner architecture. Here's why it works:

**noVNC's `vnc.html` reads the `?path=` query param** to find its WebSocket endpoint. So you point it at your Traefik-protected route instead of the same origin as the HTML file.

**Serving from Rails `public/novnc/`:**
- Rails already serves `public/` statically (zero code change needed)
- The HTML/JS is not sensitive — no auth required
- It's the same files for all sandboxes, so one copy is fine
- Browser caches it efficiently

**Traefik route becomes simpler:**
- Before: `PathPrefix(/vnc/{id})` → whole sandbox:6080 (static files + WebSocket)
- After: `PathPrefix(/vnc/{id}/websockify)` → sandbox:6080 (WebSocket only)

This means websockify-go only needs to do what it's actually good at — TCP-to-WebSocket proxying — and doesn't need any `--web` static file serving capability at all.
